接受聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗的乙型肝炎患者决策后悔及其相关危险因素研究

Prevalence of Decisional Regret and Associated Risk Factors in Patients With Hepatitis B Undergoing Pegylated Interferon Alpha Therapy.
作者： Yufen Lin, Huan Xiong, Shuanglian Yi, Shanshan Xie
摘要中文翻译背景:聚乙二醇干扰素α已被证明可提高乙型肝炎患者实现功能性治愈的可能性。然而,与核苷类似物相比,其禁忌症和不良反应数量更多。目前的研究对这种治疗方式引发患者决策后悔的潜力关注较少。目的:本研究旨在探讨接受干扰素治疗的乙型肝炎患者当前的决策后悔状况,并探索其影响因素。方法:本研究于2024年2月20日至6月13日在中国福建省厦门市的一家综合医院进行横断面研究。研究纳入了接受聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗的乙型肝炎患者。调查内容包括一般信息、健康素养全方面量表、感知社会支持量表、控制偏好量表、决策冲突量表和决策后悔量表。结果:共分析了130份问卷。仅有13.85%的患者对决策完全没有后悔,34.60%的患者经历了轻微的后悔,而中度至重度后悔的患者比例达到了51.50%。对生活的影响程度较轻、主动决策角色、被动决策角色以及决策冲突是影响乙型肝炎患者接受聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗时决策后悔的因素,这些因素共同解释了总变异的29.6%。结论:接受聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗的乙型肝炎患者决策后悔的比例较高。医疗专业人员应对此现象给予高度重视。未来可以进一步研究其他潜在的影响因素,并及时制定干预策略以有效减少决策后悔。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨接受干扰素治疗的乙型肝炎患者当前的决策后悔状况,并探索其影响因素。聚乙二醇干扰素α已被证明可提高乙型肝炎患者实现功能性治愈的可能性。
设计与对象
本研究于2024年2月20日至6月13日在中国福建省厦门市的一家综合医院进行横断面研究。研究纳入了接受聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗的乙型肝炎患者。
核心方法
调查内容包括一般信息、健康素养全方面量表、感知社会支持量表、控制偏好量表、决策冲突量表和决策后悔量表。
主要结果
共分析了130份问卷。仅有13.85%的患者对决策完全没有后悔,34.60%的患者经历了轻微的后悔,而中度至重度后悔的患者比例达到了51.50%。对生活的影响程度较轻、主动决策角色、被动决策角色以及决策冲突是影响乙型肝炎患者接受聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗时决策后悔的因素,这些因素共同解释了总变异的29.6%。
解释与创新
接受聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗的乙型肝炎患者决策后悔的比例较高。医疗专业人员应对此现象给予高度重视。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,医疗专业人员应意识到接受聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗的乙型肝炎患者决策后悔的高发生率,并考虑制定干预策略以应对这一问题。
局限与证据强度
由于研究采用横断面设计,且自我报告数据可能引入偏倚,因此该研究存在一定的局限性。由于研究的观察性质,其证据强度中等。

Prevalence of Decisional Regret and Associated Risk Factors in Patients With Hepatitis B Undergoing Pegylated Interferon Alpha Therapy.

Authors: Yufen Lin, Huan Xiong, Shuanglian Yi, Shanshan Xie
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Pegylated interferon alpha has been shown to enhance the likelihood of achieving a functional cure in patients with hepatitis B. However, it was associated with a greater number of contraindications and adverse reactions compared with nucleotide analogs. Current research has placed comparatively less emphasis on the potential for this treatment to induce decision regret among patients. AIM: The aim of this study was to investigate the current status of decision regret in hepatitis B patients undergoing interferon therapy and to explore its influencing factors. METHOD: A cross-sectional study was conducted in a comprehensive hospital in Xiamen, Fujian Province, China, from February 20 to June 13, 2024. The study recruited hepatitis B patients undergoing treatment with pegylated interferon alpha. The survey encompassed general information, the All Aspects of Health Literacy Scale, the Perceived Social Support Scale, the Control Preferences Scale, the Decisional Conflict Scale, and the Decision Regret Scale. RESULTS: A total of 130 questionnaires were analyzed. Only 13.85% of patients had no regrets at all about the decision, 34.60% experienced mild regret, and the proportion of patients with moderate to severe regrets reached 51.50%. Mild impact on life, active decision-making roles, passive decision-making roles, and decision conflict were the influencing factors of decisional regret in hepatitis B patients on pegylated interferon alpha therapy, which together explained 29.6% of the total variation. CONCLUSION: Hepatitis B patients undergoing pegylated interferon alpha therapy had a high percentage of decisional regret. Healthcare professionals should pay close attention to this phenomenon. In the future, they can investigate additional potential influencing factors and promptly develop intervention strategies to effectively reduce decision regret.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the current status of decision regret in hepatitis B patients undergoing interferon therapy and to explore its influencing factors. Pegylated interferon alpha has been shown to enhance the likelihood of achieving a functional cure in patients with hepatitis B.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in a comprehensive hospital in Xiamen, Fujian Province, China, from February 20 to June 13, 2024. The study recruited hepatitis B patients undergoing treatment with pegylated interferon alpha.
Core methods
The survey encompassed general information, the All Aspects of Health Literacy Scale, the Perceived Social Support Scale, the Control Preferences Scale, the Decisional Conflict Scale, and the Decision Regret Scale.
Main results
A total of 130 questionnaires were analyzed. Only 13.85% of patients had no regrets at all about the decision, 34.60% experienced mild regret, and the proportion of patients with moderate to severe regrets reached 51.50%. Mild impact on life, active decision-making roles, passive decision-making roles, and decision conflict were the influencing factors of decisional regret in hepatitis B patients on pegylated interferon alpha therapy, which together explained 29.6% of the total variation.
Interpretation and novelty
Hepatitis B patients undergoing pegylated interferon alpha therapy had a high percentage of decisional regret. Healthcare professionals should pay close attention to this phenomenon.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that healthcare professionals should be aware of the high prevalence of decisional regret in hepatitis B patients undergoing pegylated interferon alpha therapy and consider developing intervention strategies to address this issue.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for self-reported data to introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

ORC1在HBV相关肝细胞癌中的表达特征及其预后意义

ORC1 expression features and its prognostic significance in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma
作者： Rong Xie, Qing-Mei Li, Hang Wang, Tu-Mei Su, Qian-Bing Yin, Long Huang, Hong-Qian Liang, Yan-Fei Feng, Zi-Li Lv, Qian Wang
摘要中文翻译目的 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染是肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因。传统的生物标志物甲胎蛋白 (AFP) 和脱γ-羧基凝血酶原 (DCP) 具有诊断局限性,特别是对于早期疾病。本研究调查了 ORC1 在 HBV 相关肝病中的表达谱及其与 HBV 感染、疾病进展和预后的关系。方法 招募了 178 名参与者:124 名 HBV 阳性病例(49 名 HBV-HCC、33 名 HBV-肝硬化、42 名 慢性乙型肝炎)和 54 名 HBV 阴性对照(35 名非 HBV-HCC、19 名肝血管瘤)。通过免疫组织化学检测组织 ORC1,并通过 ELISA 检测 17 名 HBV-HCC 患者可用血清中的血清 ORC1。Logistic 回归确定了与 ORC1 高表达相关的因素。采用ROC曲线评价组织ORC1、AFP、DCP对HBV-HCC的区分度,并分析ORC1与肿瘤直径的相关性。TCGA-LIHC 数据用于外部验证。结果 HBV 阳性患者中组织 ORC1 显着上调,并且 HBV 感染与 ORC1 高表达独立相关(P < 0)。ORC1 高表达与 TCGA-LIHC 中较差的总生存期相关(HR = 1)。结论 ORC1 上调与 HBV 感染独立相关,并随着 HBV 相关肝病的疾病进展而增加。虽然组织 ORC1 对 HBV-HCC 仅表现出中度区分度,但在 TCGA-LIHC 中 ORC1 高表达与较差的总生存期相关。TCGA-LIHC。这些发现表明 ORC1 主要是 HBV 相关肝病的增殖和进展相关标志物。

这些发现表明,ORC1 主要是 HBV 相关肝病中增殖和进展相关的标志物。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于开发新的生物标志物,用于乙型肝炎相关肝细胞癌的早期检测和预后。
局限与证据强度
尽管组织 ORC1 对 HBV-HCC 仅表现出中等程度的区分,但由于样本量有限和使用外部验证数据,证据强度中等。

ORC1 expression features and its prognostic significance in HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma

Authors: Rong Xie, Qing-Mei Li, Hang Wang, Tu-Mei Su, Qian-Bing Yin, Long Huang, Hong-Qian Liang, Yan-Fei Feng, Zi-Li Lv, Qian Wang
Original abstract (English)
Objective Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a leading cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Conventional biomarkers alpha-fetoprotein (AFP) and des-gamma-carboxy prothrombin (DCP) have diagnostic limitations, particularly for early-stage disease. This study investigated the expression profile of ORC1 in HBV-related liver disease and its association with HBV infection, disease progression, and prognosis. Methods 178 participants were recruited: 124 HBV-positive cases (49 HBV-HCC, 33 HBV-cirrhosis, 42 chronic hepatitis B) and 54 HBV-negative controls (35 non-HBV-HCC, 19 hepatic hemangioma). Tissue ORC1 was detected by immunohistochemistry, and serum ORC1 was measured by ELISA in a subset of 17 HBV-HCC patients with available serum. Logistic regression identified factors associated with high ORC1 expression. ROC curves were used to evaluate the discrimination of tissue ORC1, AFP, and DCP for HBV-HCC, and the correlation between ORC1 and tumor diameter was analyzed. TCGA-LIHC data were used for external validation. Results Tissue ORC1 was significantly upregulated in HBV-positive patients, and HBV infection was independently associated with high ORC1 expression (P 0. High ORC1 expression was associated with poorer overall survival in TCGA-LIHC (HR = 1. Conclusion ORC1 upregulation is independently associated with HBV infection and increases with disease progression in HBV-related liver disease. Although tissue ORC1 showed only moderate discrimination for HBV-HCC, high ORC1 expression was associated with poorer overall survival in TCGA-LIHC. These findings suggest that ORC1 is primarily a proliferation- and progression-related marker in HBV-related liver disease.

Question and background
Objective Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a leading cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).
Design and population
This study investigated the expression profile of ORC1 in HBV-related liver disease and its association with HBV infection, disease progression, and prognosis. 178 participants were recruited: 124 HBV-positive cases (49 HBV-HCC, 33 HBV-cirrhosis, 42 chronic hepatitis B) and 54 HBV-negative controls (35 non-HBV-HCC, 19 hepatic hemangioma).

Logistic regression identified factors associated with high ORC1 expression. ROC curves were used to evaluate the discrimination of tissue ORC1, AFP, and DCP for HBV-HCC, and the correlation between ORC1 and tumor diameter was analyzed.
Main results
Tissue ORC1 was significantly upregulated in HBV-positive patients, and HBV infection was independently associated with high ORC1 expression (P 0. High ORC1 expression was associated with poorer overall survival in TCGA-LIHC (HR = 1.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that ORC1 is primarily a proliferation- and progression-related marker in HBV-related liver disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the development of new biomarkers for the early detection and prognosis of HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
Limitations and evidence strength
Although tissue ORC1 showed only moderate discrimination for HBV-HCC, the evidence is moderate in strength due to the limited sample size and the use of external validation data.

保加利亚 2021-2025 年乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎国民健康保险支出和卫生系统使用情况

National Health Insurance Expenditure and Health-System Use for Hepatitis B and C in Bulgaria, 2021–2025
作者： Kostadin Rangelov Kostadinov, Stefan Stefanov, Nikola B. Boyanov, Ani Kevorkyan, Ralitsa Raycheva, Аni Kevorkyan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在量化保加利亚国家健康保险基金 (NHIF) 抗病毒药物 报销及其价格和数量组成部分,并估计基于配药的 治疗 数量并描述服务活动。国家报销记录补充了基于模型的肝炎 治疗 成本估计。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究分析了 2021-2025 年全年的全国药房汇总记录以及 2024-2025 年全年的医院和门诊记录。该研究的重点是保加利亚与乙型和丙型肝炎相关的 抗病毒药物 报销和 治疗 数量。
核心方法
费希尔指数分解支出;具有 Newey-West 标准误差的季节性准泊松模型描述了每月趋势。诊断 识别的记录与纤维化或肝硬化代码下产品识别的 抗病毒药物 处方相结合。
主要结果
Antiviral reimbursement totalled EUR 101.5 million, including EUR 91.6 million for HCV and EUR 10.0 million for HBV. Annual antiviral reimbursement increased from EUR 18.9 million to EUR 21.7 million. The budget share for all pharmacy products in the hepatitis case definition, including adjunctive products, declined from 3.99% to 2.61%.
解释与创新
作者解释说,治疗 配药量增加,而报销单价和肝炎药物预算份额下降。这项研究为保加利亚乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎 治疗 的财务方面提供了新颖的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为保加利亚乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎 治疗 的医疗保健政策和预算规划提供信息。他们强调需要监控 治疗 数量和成本趋势,以确保有效的资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于汇总记录,并未识别独特的治疗患者。证据仅限于 2021 年至 2025 年期间,并未考虑潜在的偏差或混杂因素。研究结果的可信度为中等。

National Health Insurance Expenditure and Health-System Use for Hepatitis B and C in Bulgaria, 2021–2025

Authors: Kostadin Rangelov Kostadinov, Stefan Stefanov, Nikola B. Boyanov, Ani Kevorkyan, Ralitsa Raycheva, Аni Kevorkyan
Original abstract (English)
Background: National reimbursement records complement model-based estimates of hepatitis treatment costs. Our primary objective was to quantify Bulgarian National Health Insurance Fund (NHIF) antiviral reimbursement and its price and quantity components; secondary objectives were to estimate dispensing-based treatment volume and describe service activity. Methods: This retrospective study analysed aggregated national pharmacy records for complete years 2021–2025 and hospital and outpatient records for complete years 2024–2025. Diagnosis-identified records were combined with product-identified antiviral prescriptions under fibrosis or cirrhosis codes. Fisher indices decomposed expenditure; seasonal quasi-Poisson models with Newey–West standard errors described monthly trends. Results: Antiviral reimbursement totalled EUR 101.5 million, including EUR 91.6 million for HCV and EUR 10.0 million for HBV. Annual antiviral reimbursement increased from EUR 18.9 million to EUR 21.7 million. The budget share for all pharmacy products in the hepatitis case definition, including adjunctive products, declined from 3.99% to 2.61%. Relative to 2021, the 2025 Fisher price and quantity indices were 0.799 and 1.395 for HBV and 0.885 and 1.447 for HCV. Estimated mean continuous HBV therapy increased from 2226 to 3051 people; HCV standard-course equivalents increased from 604 to 777, with duration-based ranges of 431–607 and 571–777. These estimates do not identify unique treated patients. Annualised inpatient-episode trends during 2024–2025 were 9.2% for HBV and −7.4% for HCV; consultation trends were 3.7% and −6.9%. Conclusions: Dispensed treatment volume increased while reimbursed unit prices and the hepatitis pharmaceutical budget share declined. These findings describe payer expenditure and encounters; they do not establish improved affordability, access, or treatment coverage.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to quantify Bulgarian National Health Insurance Fund (NHIF) antiviral reimbursement and its price and quantity components, as well as estimate dispensing-based treatment volume and describe service activity. National reimbursement records complement model-based estimates of hepatitis treatment costs.
Design and population
This retrospective study analysed aggregated national pharmacy records for complete years 2021–2025 and hospital and outpatient records for complete years 2024–2025. The study focused on antiviral reimbursement and treatment volume related to hepatitis B and C in Bulgaria.
Core methods
Fisher indices decomposed expenditure; seasonal quasi-Poisson models with Newey–West standard errors described monthly trends. Diagnosis-identified records were combined with product-identified antiviral prescriptions under fibrosis or cirrhosis codes.
Main results
Antiviral reimbursement totalled EUR 101.5 million, including EUR 91.6 million for HCV and EUR 10.0 million for HBV. Annual antiviral reimbursement increased from EUR 18.9 million to EUR 21.7 million. The budget share for all pharmacy products in the hepatitis case definition, including adjunctive products, declined from 3.99% to 2.61%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that dispensed treatment volume increased while reimbursed unit prices and the hepatitis pharmaceutical budget share declined. This study provides novel insights into the financial aspects of hepatitis B and C treatment in Bulgaria.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform healthcare policy and budget planning for hepatitis B and C treatment in Bulgaria. They highlight the need for monitoring treatment volume and cost trends to ensure effective resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on aggregated records and does not identify unique treated patients. The evidence is limited to the period 2021–2025 and does not account for potential biases or confounding factors. The confidence in the findings is moderate.

乙型肝炎病毒 感染中 HBV DNA 水平与肝脏相关参数和非侵入性肝脏评分的关联:沙特阿拉伯的一项回顾性横断面研究

Associations of HBV DNA Level with Liver-Related Parameters and Non-Invasive Liver Scores in Hepatitis B Virus Infection: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study in Saudi Arabia
作者： Abdulrahman F. Alrezaihi, Lama Alzamil, Nami M. Aljehani, Hala Aldahshan, Fuad Alanazi, Abdulaziz M. Almuqrin
摘要中文翻译背景和目标:乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全世界进行性肝病的主要原因。尽管 HBV DNA 反映了病毒复制,但其与传统肝脏生化标志物和非侵入性指标的关系在沙特患者中的研究仍不充分。本研究评估了 HBV DNA 水平与常规肝脏相关参数、白蛋白胆红素 (ALBI) 评分和纤维化 4 (FIB-4) 指数的关联。材料和方法:这项回顾性横断面研究包括 2015 年 1 月至 2026 年 6 月期间在哈立德国王大学医院评估的 HBV 感染患者。记录 诊断 日期后 12 个月内收集的实验室数据用于计算 ALBI 和 FIB-4 评分。使用 Spearman 相关性和基于 2000 IU/mL 的预定义阈值的组比较来评估 HBV DNA 和肝脏相关参数之间的关系。多变量逻辑回归评估了与 ALBI 2-3 级相关的因素。结果:在 394 名患者中,中位肝酶水平总体上在参考范围内,尽管有可用数据的患者中 55.7% 的 ALBI 为 2-3 级。在 160 名具有可量化 HBV DNA 的患者中,病毒载量与完整分析 队列研究 中所有七个评估的肝脏相关参数显着相关,其中 AST 显示最强的关联(ρ = 0.437,p < 0.001),尽管敏感性分析表明这些关联可能部分反映了 治疗 相关的病毒载量分布差异。HBV DNA ≥2000 IU/mL 的患者与低于此阈值的患者的肝脏相关参数显着不同。HBV DNA 每增加十倍,ALBI 2-3 级的几率就会增加 69%(OR = 1.69;95% CI:1.32-2.25;p < 0.001)。ALBI 和 FIB-4 呈中度正相关(ρ = 0.392,p < 0.001)。结论:较高的 HBV DNA 水平与肝脏相关参数的差异和较低的 ALBI 等级相关。将病毒载量测量与常规标记物和非侵入性指标相结合,可以对 HBV 感染中的肝脏受累情况进行更全面的评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全世界进行性肝病的主要原因。尽管 HBV DNA 反映了病毒复制,但其与传统肝脏生化标志物和非侵入性指标的关系在沙特患者中的研究仍不充分。
设计与对象
这项回顾性横断面研究纳入了 2015 年 1 月至 2026 年 6 月期间在哈立德国王大学医院评估的 HBV 感染患者。在 394 名患者中,中位肝酶水平总体在参考范围内,尽管有可用数据的患者中 55.7% 的 ALBI 为 2-3 级。
核心方法
使用 Spearman 相关性和基于 2000 IU/mL 的预定义阈值的组比较来评估 HBV DNA 和肝脏相关参数之间的关系。多变量逻辑回归评估了与 ALBI 2-3 级相关的因素。
主要结果
在 160 名具有可量化 HBV DNA 的患者中,病毒载量与完整分析 队列研究 中所有七个评估的肝脏相关参数显着相关,其中 AST 显示出最强的相关性(ρ = 0.437,p < 0.001)。HBV DNA ≥2000 IU/mL 的患者与低于此阈值的患者的肝脏相关参数显着不同。
解释与创新
较高的 HBV DNA 水平与肝脏相关参数的差异和较低的 ALBI 等级相关。将病毒载量测量与常规标记物和非侵入性指标相结合,可以对 HBV 感染中的肝脏受累情况进行更全面的评估。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为使用 HBV DNA 水平作为评估肝脏参与 HBV 感染的补充工具提供信息,从而有可能改进非侵入性诊断方法和监测策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和病毒载量分布中与 治疗 相关的潜在差异而受到限制,这可能会影响关联的解释。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Associations of HBV DNA Level with Liver-Related Parameters and Non-Invasive Liver Scores in Hepatitis B Virus Infection: A Retrospective Cross-Sectional Study in Saudi Arabia

Authors: Abdulrahman F. Alrezaihi, Lama Alzamil, Nami M. Aljehani, Hala Aldahshan, Fuad Alanazi, Abdulaziz M. Almuqrin
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objectives: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major cause of progressive liver disease worldwide. Although HBV DNA reflects viral replication, its relationships with conventional liver biochemical markers and non-invasive indices remain insufficiently studied in Saudi patients. This study evaluated the associations of HBV DNA level with conventional liver-related parameters, the Albumin–Bilirubin (ALBI) score, and the Fibrosis-4 (FIB-4) index. Materials and Methods: This retrospective cross-sectional study included patients with HBV infection evaluated at King Khalid University Hospital between January 2015 and June 2026. Laboratory data collected within 12 months of the recorded diagnosis date were used to calculate ALBI and FIB-4 scores. Relationships between HBV DNA and liver-related parameters were evaluated using Spearman correlation and group comparisons based on a predefined threshold of 2000 IU/mL. Multivariable logistic regression assessed factors associated with ALBI Grades 2–3. Results: Among 394 patients, median liver enzyme levels were generally within their reference ranges, although 55.7% of patients with available data had ALBI Grades 2–3. Among 160 patients with quantifiable HBV DNA, viral load correlated significantly with all seven evaluated liver-related parameters in the full analytical cohort, with AST showing the strongest association (ρ = 0.437, p < 0.001), although sensitivity analyses suggested these associations may partly reflect treatment-related differences in viral load distribution. Patients with HBV DNA ≥2000 IU/mL had significantly different liver-related parameters than those below this threshold. Each tenfold increase in HBV DNA was associated with 69% higher odds of ALBI Grades 2–3 (OR = 1.69; 95% CI: 1.32–2.25; p < 0.001). ALBI and FIB-4 were moderately positively correlated (ρ = 0.392, p < 0.001). Conclusions: Higher HBV DNA levels were associated with differences in liver-related parameters and poorer ALBI grade. Combining viral-load measurement with conventional markers and non-invasive indices may provide a more comprehensive assessment of hepatic involvement in HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major cause of progressive liver disease worldwide. Although HBV DNA reflects viral replication, its relationships with conventional liver biochemical markers and non-invasive indices remain insufficiently studied in Saudi patients.
Design and population
This retrospective cross-sectional study included patients with HBV infection evaluated at King Khalid University Hospital between January 2015 and June 2026. Main results

Patients with HBV DNA ≥2000 IU/mL had significantly different liver-related parameters than those below this threshold.

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the use of HBV DNA levels as a complementary tool in assessing liver involvement in HBV infection, potentially improving non-invasive diagnostic approaches and monitoring strategies.

The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential treatment-related differences in viral load distribution, which may affect the interpretation of associations. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性与肿瘤 发病率 之间的关联:中国南方地区的一项大型前瞻性 队列研究 研究

Association Between Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Positivity and Tumor Incidence: A Large Prospective Cohort Study in Southern China
作者： Qi-Lan Wen, Yu Wang, Yi-Kun Chang, Jin-Wei Chen, Na Cao, Xin-Gui Sun, Yuan-Tao Hao, Wang-Jian Zhang, Zhi-Cheng Du
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染是全球主要的健康问题,并与致癌作用有关。然而,关于乙肝表面抗原(HBsAg)阳性与总体和特定部位肿瘤风险之间关联的证据仍然有限。本研究旨在探讨HBsAg阳性与肿瘤风险在一项大型前瞻性队列研究中的关联。方法:这项前瞻性队列研究包括2009年至2015年间在中国南方招募的538,573名参与者。通过与医院出院数据库链接,识别出新记录的肿瘤诊断,该数据库可追溯至2022年12月。缺失的协变量值采用五次多重插补方法处理,并在每个数据集中分别进行倾向评分匹配。Cox模型估计值采用Rubin规则进行合并。同时进行了时间变化分析和地标分析。结果:基线HBsAg阳性与所有部位肿瘤风险增加相关(合并风险比[HR]为2.02;95%置信区间[CI]为1.79–2.27),消化系统恶性肿瘤(合并HR为4.28;95% CI为3.51–5.22)以及良性肿瘤(合并HR为1.52;95% CI为1.27–1.82)相关。肝癌和肝内胆管恶性肿瘤表现出强烈的关联(合并HR为15.49;95% CI为8.99–26.67),以及肝细胞癌(合并HR为19.78;95% CI为7.19–54.44)相关。相比之下,与消化系统恶性肿瘤(非肝部)的关联没有统计学意义。这些关联通常在随访过程中减弱,但在排除前1年和2年内记录的肿瘤事件后仍持续存在。所有部位肿瘤的关联在男性和年龄≥45岁的参与者中更为显著。结论:基线HBsAg阳性与总体和特定部位肿瘤风险增加相关,尤其是消化系统恶性肿瘤。这些关联在男性和老年人中更为明显。这些发现支持对HBsAg阳性个体进行进一步的临床评估,并在临床需要时依据指南进行肝癌监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染是全球主要的健康问题,并与癌症发生有关。然而,关于乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阳性与整体和特定部位肿瘤风险之间关系的证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究纳入了 2009 年至 2015 年间在中国南方招募的 538,573 名参与者。通过与截至 2022 年 12 月的出院数据库连接,确定了新记录的肿瘤诊断。
核心方法
使用五个多重插补来解决缺失的协变量值,并在每个数据集中单独执行倾向得分匹配。考克斯模型的估计是使用鲁宾规则组合的。还进行了时变分析和里程碑分析。
主要结果
Baseline HBsAg positivity was associated with increased risks of all-site tumors (pooled hazard ratio [HR], 2.02; 95% confidence interval [CI], 1.79–2.27), digestive system malignancies (pooled HR, 4.28; 95% CI, 3.51–5.22), and benign tumors (pooled HR, 1.52; 95% CI, 1.27–1.82). Strong associations were observed for liver and intrahepatic bile duct malignancies (pooled HR, 15.49; 95% CI, 8.99–26.67) and hepatocellular carcinoma (pooled HR, 19.78; 95% CI, 7.19–54.44). The associations generally attenuated over follow-up but persisted after excluding tumor events recorded within the first 1 and 2 years. Stronger associations with all-site tumors were observed among men and participants aged ≥45 years.
解释与创新
基线 HBsAg 阳性与整体和特定部位肿瘤(特别是消化系统恶性肿瘤)的风险增加相关。这种关联在男性和老年人中更为明显。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在有临床指征时对 HBsAg 阳性个体和基于指南的肝癌 监测 进行进一步的临床评估。
局限与证据强度
由于潜在的残留混杂因素以及肿瘤诊断依赖于出院数据,证据是中等的。

Association Between Hepatitis B Surface Antigen Positivity and Tumor Incidence: A Large Prospective Cohort Study in Southern China

Authors: Qi-Lan Wen, Yu Wang, Yi-Kun Chang, Jin-Wei Chen, Na Cao, Xin-Gui Sun, Yuan-Tao Hao, Wang-Jian Zhang, Zhi-Cheng Du
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a major global health concern and has been implicated in carcinogenesis. However, evidence regarding the association between hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) positivity and the risk of overall and site-specific tumors remains limited. This study aimed to investigate the association between HBsAg positivity and tumor incidence in a large prospective cohort. Methods: This prospective cohort study included 538,573 participants recruited in southern China between 2009 and 2015. Newly recorded tumor diagnoses were identified by linkage to a hospital discharge database available through December 2022. Missing covariate values were addressed using five multiple imputations, and propensity score matching was performed separately within each dataset. Cox model estimates were combined using Rubin’s rules. Time-varying and landmark analyses were also performed. Results: Baseline HBsAg positivity was associated with increased risks of all-site tumors (pooled hazard ratio [HR], 2.02; 95% confidence interval [CI], 1.79–2.27), digestive system malignancies (pooled HR, 4.28; 95% CI, 3.51–5.22), and benign tumors (pooled HR, 1.52; 95% CI, 1.27–1.82). Strong associations were observed for liver and intrahepatic bile duct malignancies (pooled HR, 15.49; 95% CI, 8.99–26.67) and hepatocellular carcinoma (pooled HR, 19.78; 95% CI, 7.19–54.44). In contrast, the association with extrahepatic digestive system malignancies was not statistically significant. The associations generally attenuated over follow-up but persisted after excluding tumor events recorded within the first 1 and 2 years. Stronger associations with all-site tumors were observed among men and participants aged ≥45 years. Conclusions: Baseline HBsAg positivity was associated with an increased risk of overall and site-specific tumors, particularly digestive system malignancies. The associations were stronger among men and older adults. These findings support further clinical evaluation of HBsAg-positive individuals and guideline-based liver cancer surveillance when clinically indicated.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a major global health concern and has been implicated in carcinogenesis. However, evidence regarding the association between hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) positivity and the risk of overall and site-specific tumors remains limited.
Design and population
This prospective cohort study included 538,573 participants recruited in southern China between 2009 and 2015. Newly recorded tumor diagnoses were identified by linkage to a hospital discharge database available through December 2022.
Core methods
Missing covariate values were addressed using five multiple imputations, and propensity score matching was performed separately within each dataset. Cox model estimates were combined using Rubin’s rules. Time-varying and landmark analyses were also performed.
Main results
Baseline HBsAg positivity was associated with increased risks of all-site tumors (pooled hazard ratio [HR], 2.02; 95% confidence interval [CI], 1.79–2.27), digestive system malignancies (pooled HR, 4.28; 95% CI, 3.51–5.22), and benign tumors (pooled HR, 1.52; 95% CI, 1.27–1.82). Strong associations were observed for liver and intrahepatic bile duct malignancies (pooled HR, 15.49; 95% CI, 8.99–26.67) and hepatocellular carcinoma (pooled HR, 19.78; 95% CI, 7.19–54.44). The associations generally attenuated over follow-up but persisted after excluding tumor events recorded within the first 1 and 2 years. Stronger associations with all-site tumors were observed among men and participants aged ≥45 years.
Interpretation and novelty
Baseline HBsAg positivity was associated with an increased risk of overall and site-specific tumors, particularly digestive system malignancies. The associations were stronger among men and older adults.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support further clinical evaluation of HBsAg-positive individuals and guideline-based liver cancer surveillance when clinically indicated.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is moderate due to the potential for residual confounding and the reliance on hospital discharge data for tumor diagnoses.

肯尼亚沿海地区 HIV 感染者和未感染注射吸毒者中 5 个 乙型肝炎病毒 标记的分布

DISTRIBUTION OF FIVE HEPATITIS B VIRUS MARKERS IN HIV INFECTED AND UNINFECTED INJECTING DRUG USERS IN COASTAL KENYA
作者： Mark Kilongosi Webale
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究确定了来自肯尼亚蒙巴萨的 HIV-1 感染者 (n=157) 和未感染者 (n=214) 的注射吸毒者中的 HBV 血清标志物谱。HIV-1 诊断 使用DetermineTM 和 UnigoldTM 快速检测进行。方法:使用 FacsCalibur 流式细胞仪对 CD4+ T 细胞进行计数,并根据疾病控制和预防中心 (CDC) 免疫分期标准进行分类。使用 HBV-5 组快速检测盒测定 HBV 血清谱。临床分期基于五种 HBV 标志物的血清学特征。结果:与未感染者相比,HIV-1感染者的HBsAg(P=0.004)和HBcAb-IgM(P=0.008)阳性率较高,而HBsAb阳性率则相反(P=0.019)。与未感染者相比,HIV-1 感染者的急性(P=0.033)和慢性(P=0.021)临床分期率较高,但 疫苗 型反应率则相反(P=0.008)。与未感染者 (n=4) 相比,在 HIV-1 感染者 (n=12) 中仅检测到基因型 A,亚基因型 A1 的频率更高。结论:与肯尼亚蒙巴萨未感染的注射吸毒者相比,HIV-1 感染者的 HBsAg 和 HBcAb-IgM 血清标志物以及急性和慢性临床类别较高,而 HBsAb 血清标志物和 疫苗 型反应阶段较低。总而言之,感染 HIV 的注射吸毒者有感染 HBV 并发展为慢性感染的风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定肯尼亚蒙巴萨 HIV-1 感染者和未感染注射吸毒者 (IDU) 中 5 个 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 标记物的分布,以了解该人群中 HIV 和 HBV 感染之间的关系。
设计与对象
2023 年至 2024 年间,在肯尼亚蒙巴萨对 157 名 HIV-1 感染者和 214 名未感染的注射吸毒者进行了一项横断面研究。使用 DefineTM 和 UnigoldTM 快速检测进行 HIV-1 诊断。
核心方法
使用 HBV-5 组快速检测盒测定 HBV 血清谱。使用 FacsCalibur 流式细胞仪对 CD4+ T 细胞进行计数,并根据 CDC 免疫分期标准进行分类。临床分期基于五种 HBV 标志物的血清学特征。
主要结果
与未感染者相比,HIV-1 感染者的 HBsAg (P=0.004) 和 HBcAb-IgM (P=0.008) 阳性率较高,而 HBsAb 阳性率较低 (P=0.019)。HIV-1感染者的急性(P=0.033)和慢性(P=0.021)临床分期率较高,但疫苗型缓解率较低(P=0.008)。仅检测到基因型 A、亚基因型 A1,与未感染者 (n=4) 相比,HIV-1 感染者 (n=12) 中出现频率较高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HIV-1 感染与 HBV 感染和进展为慢性感染的风险增加有关,而该组中的 疫苗 反应减弱。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了监测 HIV-1 感染的注射吸毒者的 HBV 标志物的重要性,以指导有针对性的预防和 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横断面设计和对快速测试的依赖而受到限制,而快速测试的敏感性可能较低。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

DISTRIBUTION OF FIVE HEPATITIS B VIRUS MARKERS IN HIV INFECTED AND UNINFECTED INJECTING DRUG USERS IN COASTAL KENYA

Authors: Mark Kilongosi Webale
Original abstract (English)
Aims: This study determined HBV sero-markers profile in HIV-1 infected (n=157) and uninfected (n=214) IDUs from Mombasa, Kenya HIV-1 diagnosis was performed using DetermineTM and UnigoldTM rapid tests. Methodology: The CD4+ T cells were enumerated using the FacsCalibur flow cytometer and categorized according to the Centre for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) immunological staging criteria. HBV seroprofile was determined using HBV-5 panel rapid test cassettes. Clinical staging was based on serological profile of the five HBV markers. Results: HBsAg (P=0.004) and HBcAb-IgM (P=0.008) positivities were higher in the HIV-1 infected compared to the uninfected individuals while the opposite was observed for HBsAb positivity (P=0.019). Rates of acute (P=0.033) and chronic (P=0.021) clinical stages were higher in the HIV-1 infected compared to the uninfected individuals but the inverse was noticed for vaccine type response rate (P=0.008). Only genotype A, sub-genotype A1 was detected with higher frequencies noted in the HIV-1 infected (n=12) compared to the uninfected (n=4) individuals. Conclusion: HBsAg and HBcAb-IgM sero-markers and acute and chronic clinical categories are higher while HBsAb sero-marker and vaccine type response stage are lower in HIV-1 infected compared to uninfected IDUs at Mombasa, Kenya. Taken together, HIV infected injection drug users are at risk of acquiring HBV and progressing to chronic infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to determine the distribution of five hepatitis B virus (HBV) markers in HIV-1 infected and uninfected injecting drug users (IDUs) in Mombasa, Kenya, to understand the relationship between HIV and HBV infections in this population.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 157 HIV-1 infected and 214 uninfected injecting drug users in Mombasa, Kenya, between 2023 and 2024. HIV-1 diagnosis was performed using DetermineTM and UnigoldTM rapid tests.
Core methods
HBV seroprofile was determined using HBV-5 panel rapid test cassettes. CD4+ T cells were enumerated using the FacsCalibur flow cytometer and categorized according to CDC immunological staging criteria. Clinical staging was based on the serological profile of the five HBV markers.
Main results
HBsAg (P=0.004) and HBcAb-IgM (P=0.008) positivities were higher in HIV-1 infected compared to uninfected individuals, while HBsAb positivity was lower (P=0.019). Rates of acute (P=0.033) and chronic (P=0.021) clinical stages were higher in HIV-1 infected individuals, but vaccine type response rate was lower (P=0.008). Only genotype A, sub-genotype A1 was detected, with higher frequencies in HIV-1 infected individuals (n=12) compared to uninfected (n=4).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HIV-1 infection is associated with increased risk of HBV acquisition and progression to chronic infection, while vaccine response is diminished in this group.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of monitoring HBV markers in HIV-1 infected injecting drug users to inform targeted prevention and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and reliance on rapid tests, which may have lower sensitivity. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

自行停止短期布列韦肽治疗后持续丁型肝炎病毒核糖核酸阴性:病例报告

Sustained hepatitis D virus-ribonucleic acid negativity after self-discontinuation of short-term bulevirtide therapy: a case report
作者： Giovanni Massobrio, Alessandro Limongelli, Laura Ambra Nicolini, Alessandro Loglio, Marco Nofri, Salvatore Cardaci, Paolo Borro, Valentina Ricucci, Serena Varesano, Antonio Di Biagio
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 慢性丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)感染代表最严重的病毒性肝炎形式,与加速进展至肝硬化、肝功能失代偿和肝细胞癌相关。Bulevirtide(BLV)是一种针对NTCP受体的进入抑制剂,最近已成为慢性HDV感染的标准治疗方案治疗。尽管其在临床试验和真实世界研究中已显示出疗效和安全性,但最佳治疗持续时间和治疗停药标准仍未明确。病例报告 我们报告了一例60岁男性患者的病例,该患者长期患有HIV/HBV/HDV共感染,并曾于1993年成功治疗过丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)感染。他之前接受了两疗程的聚乙二醇干扰素α治疗,但未达到病毒学反应。在开始使用tenofovir alafenamide/emtricitabine/rilpivirine治疗人类免疫缺陷病毒(HIV)和乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染的同时,开始使用BLV单药治疗(2 mg/天)。由于轻度头痛和肌痛,治疗在6个月后被停用。尽管早期治疗中断,但药物停用后HDV-RNA仍持续下降,并在10个月后检测不到。病毒抑制在多次评估中得到确认,并在近1年的随访中保持检测不到状态。在此期间,肝酶水平保持在正常范围内,定量HBsAg逐渐下降,肝硬度从14降至7.8 kPa。结论 该病例展示了在非肝硬化且HIV和HBV感染得到控制的个体中,短期使用BLV单药治疗后持续HDV-RNA抑制的罕见例子。治疗停药后病毒载量的持续下降可能反映了由宿主介导的HDV复制免疫控制,可能受到低HBsAg水平的促进。由于HBsAg水平下降先于BLV治疗开始,因此自发清除和BLV相关效应仍是竞争性解释。尽管这一单一观察结果不支持在临床实践中缩短BLV治疗时间,但它强调了在治疗停药后识别持久病毒学控制预测因子的必要性,以指导更具个性化的治疗策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染是最严重的病毒性肝炎,与加速发展为肝硬化、肝功能失代偿和肝细胞癌有关。该研究旨在探讨短期 bulevirtide 治疗后 HDV-RNA 持续阴性的可能性。
设计与对象
病例报告 描述了一名 60 岁男性,长期感染 HIV/HBV/HDV,之前曾感染过丙型肝炎病毒,并于 1993 年成功治疗。他之前接受了两个疗程的聚乙二醇化干扰素 α,但未达到病毒学应答。
核心方法
布勒韦肽 (BLV) 单药治疗(2 毫克/天)与替诺福韦艾拉酚胺/恩曲他滨/利匹韦林一起开始治疗人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV) 和 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染。治疗 因轻度头痛和肌痛在 6 个月后停药。
主要结果
尽管早期治疗中断,但药物停用后HDV-RNA仍持续下降,并在10个月后检测不到。病毒抑制在多次评估中得到确认,并在近1年的随访中保持检测不到状态。
解释与创新
治疗 停药后病毒载量持续下降可能反映了 宿主 介导的 HDV 复制的免疫控制,这可能受到低 HBsAg 水平的支持。该病例是一个罕见的例子,在 HIV 和 HBV 感染得到控制的非肝硬化个体中,短期 BLV 单一疗法后 HDV-RNA 持续受到抑制。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调需要确定 治疗 停药后持久病毒学控制的预测因素,以指导针对慢性 HDV 感染的更个体化的 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
虽然这一单一观察结果并不支持在临床实践中缩短 BLV 治疗,但它提供了一个罕见的例子,可以为未来 HDV 复制免疫控制的研究提供信息。证据仅限于单个 病例报告,可能无法推广。

Sustained hepatitis D virus-ribonucleic acid negativity after self-discontinuation of short-term bulevirtide therapy: a case report

Authors: Giovanni Massobrio, Alessandro Limongelli, Laura Ambra Nicolini, Alessandro Loglio, Marco Nofri, Salvatore Cardaci, Paolo Borro, Valentina Ricucci, Serena Varesano, Antonio Di Biagio
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Chronic hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection represents the most severe form of viral hepatitis and is associated with accelerated progression to cirrhosis, hepatic decompensation, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Bulevirtide (BLV), an entry inhibitor targeting the NTCP receptor, has recently emerged as the standard treatment for chronic HDV infection. Although its efficacy and safety have been demonstrated in clinical trials and real-world studies, the optimal duration of therapy and criteria for treatment discontinuation remain undefined. Case report We report the case of a 60-year-old man with long-standing HIV/HBV/HDV coinfection and previous hepatitis C virus infection successfully treated in 1993. He had previously undergone two courses of pegylated interferon alpha without achieving virological response. BLV monotherapy (2 mg/day) was started alongside tenofovir alafenamide/emtricitabine/rilpivirine for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) and hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. Treatment was discontinued after 6 months due to mild headache and myalgia. Despite early treatment interruption, HDV-RNA continued to decline after drug withdrawal, becoming undetectable 10 months later. Viral suppression was confirmed in multiple assessments and remained undetectable for nearly 1 year of follow-up. Throughout this period, liver enzymes remained within the normal range, quantitative HBsAg progressively declined, and liver stiffness decreased from 14 to 7.8 kPa. Conclusions This case illustrates a rare example of sustained HDV-RNA suppression after short-term BLV monotherapy in a non-cirrhotic individual with controlled HIV and HBV infection. The continued decline in viral load after treatment discontinuation may reflect a host mediated-immune control of HDV replication, possibly favored by low HBsAg levels. As the decline in HBsAg preceded BLV initiation, spontaneous clearance and a BLV-associated effect remain competing explanations. While this single observation does not support shortening BLV therapy in clinical practice, it highlights the need to identify predictors of durable virological control after treatment withdrawal to guide more individualized treatment strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection represents the most severe form of viral hepatitis and is associated with accelerated progression to cirrhosis, hepatic decompensation, and hepatocellular carcinoma. The study aimed to explore the possibility of sustained HDV-RNA negativity after short-term bulevirtide therapy.
Design and population
A case report describes a 60-year-old man with long-standing HIV/HBV/HDV coinfection and previous hepatitis C virus infection successfully treated in 1993. He had previously undergone two courses of pegylated interferon alpha without achieving virological response.
Core methods
Bulevirtide (BLV) monotherapy (2 mg/day) was started alongside tenofovir alafenamide/emtricitabine/rilpivirine for human immunodeficiency virus (HIV) and hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. Treatment was discontinued after 6 months due to mild headache and myalgia.
Main results
Despite early treatment interruption, HDV-RNA continued to decline after drug withdrawal, becoming undetectable 10 months later. Viral suppression was confirmed in multiple assessments and remained undetectable for nearly 1 year of follow-up.
Interpretation and novelty
The continued decline in viral load after treatment discontinuation may reflect a host mediated-immune control of HDV replication, possibly favored by low HBsAg levels. This case illustrates a rare example of sustained HDV-RNA suppression after short-term BLV monotherapy in a non-cirrhotic individual with controlled HIV and HBV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case highlights the need to identify predictors of durable virological control after treatment withdrawal to guide more individualized treatment strategies for chronic HDV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
While this single observation does not support shortening BLV therapy in clinical practice, it provides a rare example that may inform future studies on immune control of HDV replication. The evidence is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable.

阿萨巴联邦医疗中心产前参加者对乙型和丙型病毒性肝炎的认识、危险因素和 患病率:一项横断面研究

Awareness, Risk Factors and Prevalence of Viral Hepatitis B and C among Antenatal Attendees in Federal Medical Centre, Asaba: A Cross-sectional Study
作者： Sylvia Ifeoma Obu, Anthony Uchenna Asiodu, Ekeneam Omo, James Chukwunenye Ibuaku, Francis Chinedu Ugoeze, Anastacia Okwudili Ojimba, Mfonobong Edward Effiong, Regimus Azuka Obi, Samuel Okwuchukwu Ilikannu, Ndudi Dibigo–Ibeaji
摘要中文翻译目的:评估 Asaba 联邦医疗中心的产前参加者对乙型和丙型病毒性肝炎的认识水平、危险因素和 患病率。研究地点和持续时间:三角洲州阿萨巴联邦医疗中心,2025 年 6 月至 10 月。方法:这项基于医院的描述性横断面研究涉及 2025 年 6 月至 10 月在阿萨巴联邦医疗中心产前诊所就诊的 145 名孕妇。研究开始前获得了伦理批准,并在入组前获得了所有参与者的知情同意。使用预先测试的结构化问卷收集了参与者的社会人口学特征、意识水平、知识以及与乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎感染相关的可能危险因素的数据。从每位参与者身上无菌采集约 3 mL 静脉血,并使用标准快速诊断检测试剂盒分析乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和丙型肝炎病毒抗体。使用 IBM SPSS Statistics 并应用描述性和推论性统计方法对获得的数据进行分析。统计显着性设定为 p ≤ 0.05。结果:到产前门诊就诊的145名孕妇中,大多数年龄在21岁至30岁之间(72.4%),几乎全部已婚(96.6%)。对乙型肝炎的认知度较高,约四分之三的参与者听说过它(75.2%),而对丙型肝炎的认知度较低(58.6%)。总体而言,参与者对这些感染的传播方式有一定的了解(52.4%)。医疗保健专业人员是最常报告的病毒性肝炎信息来源(46.9%)。尽管大部分女性承认乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的重要性(77.9%),但只有少数女性接受过 疫苗(13.1%)。筛查结果显示,2.8% 的参与者乙型肝炎检测呈阳性,而没有人丙型肝炎检测呈阳性。结论:研究结果表明,尽管孕妇对病毒性肝炎的认识相当令人鼓舞,但乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 的低摄取率仍然是一个主要问题。这凸显了加强产前检查、定期筛查和改进预防措施以减轻孕妇肝炎感染负担的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 Asaba 联邦医疗中心的产前参加者对乙型病毒性肝炎和丙型病毒性肝炎的认识、危险因素和 患病率,以解决妊娠期间 传播 型肝炎的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
在三角洲州阿萨巴联邦医疗中心进行了一项以医院为基础的描述性横断面研究,研究对象为 2025 年 6 月至 10 月期间到产前诊所就诊的 145 名孕妇。
核心方法
使用预先测试的结构化问卷收集有关社会人口特征、意识、知识和风险因素的数据。收集血样并使用快速诊断检测试剂盒检测乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 和丙型肝炎病毒抗体。使用 IBM SPSS Statistics 通过描述性和推论性统计方法对数据进行分析。
主要结果
在145名参与者中,72.4%的人年龄在21-30岁,其中96.6%已婚。乙型肝炎的知晓率为75.2%,丙型肝炎的知晓率为58.6%。只有 13.1% 的参与者曾感染过乙型肝炎 疫苗接种。筛查结果显示,2.8% 的人乙型肝炎检测呈阳性,丙型肝炎没有呈阳性。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,虽然对乙型肝炎的认识相对较高,但 疫苗接种 的低吸收率仍然是一个主要问题,这凸显了改进健康教育和预防措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为产前检查期间有针对性的健康教育计划提供信息,并强调定期筛查和疫苗接种对于减轻孕妇乙型肝炎感染负担的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于其横截面设计和小样本量,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Awareness, Risk Factors and Prevalence of Viral Hepatitis B and C among Antenatal Attendees in Federal Medical Centre, Asaba: A Cross-sectional Study

Authors: Sylvia Ifeoma Obu, Anthony Uchenna Asiodu, Ekeneam Omo, James Chukwunenye Ibuaku, Francis Chinedu Ugoeze, Anastacia Okwudili Ojimba, Mfonobong Edward Effiong, Regimus Azuka Obi, Samuel Okwuchukwu Ilikannu, Ndudi Dibigo–Ibeaji
Original abstract (English)
Aim: To evaluate the level of awareness, risk factors, and prevalence of viral hepatitis B and C among antenatal attendees at Federal Medical Centre, Asaba. Place and Duration of Study: Federal Medical Centre, Asaba, Delta State, from June to October 2025. Methodology: This hospital-based descriptive cross-sectional study involved 145 pregnant women attending the antenatal clinic at Federal Medical Centre, Asaba, from June to October 2025. Ethical approval was obtained before the commencement of the study, and informed consent was secured from all participants prior to enrolment. Data on participants’ sociodemographic characteristics, level of awareness, knowledge, and possible risk factors associated with hepatitis B and C infection were collected using a pretested structured questionnaire. Approximately 3 mL of venous blood was collected aseptically from each participant and analysed for hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and hepatitis C virus antibodies using standard rapid diagnostic test kits. The data obtained were analysed using IBM SPSS Statistics, with both descriptive and inferential statistical methods applied. Statistical significance was set at p ≤ 0.05. Results: Of the 145 pregnant women who attended the antenatal clinic, most were between 21 and 30 years old (72.4%), and almost all were married (96.6%). Awareness of hepatitis B was relatively high, with about three-quarters of participants having heard of it (75.2%), while awareness of hepatitis C was lower (58.6%). Overall, participants demonstrated a moderate understanding of how these infections are transmitted (52.4%). Healthcare professionals were the most commonly reported source of information on viral hepatitis (46.9%). Although a large proportion of the women acknowledged the importance of hepatitis B vaccination (77.9%), only a small number had received the vaccine (13.1%). Screening results showed that 2.8% of the participants tested positive for hepatitis B, while none tested positive for hepatitis C. Conclusion: The findings indicate that although awareness of viral hepatitis among pregnant women was fairly encouraging, the low uptake of hepatitis B vaccination remains a major concern. This highlights the need for stronger health education during antenatal visits, regular screening, and improved preventive measures to reduce the burden of hepatitis infections among pregnant women.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess awareness, risk factors, and prevalence of viral hepatitis B and C among antenatal attendees at Federal Medical Centre, Asaba, to address the public health concern of hepatitis transmission during pregnancy.
Design and population
A hospital-based descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted at Federal Medical Centre, Asaba, Delta State, involving 145 pregnant women attending the antenatal clinic from June to October 2025.
Core methods
Data on sociodemographic characteristics, awareness, knowledge, and risk factors were collected using a pretested structured questionnaire. Blood samples were collected and tested for hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and hepatitis C virus antibodies using rapid diagnostic test kits. Data were analysed using IBM SPSS Statistics with descriptive and inferential statistical methods.
Main results
Of the 145 participants, 72.4% were aged 21-30 years, and 96.6% were married. Awareness of hepatitis B was 75.2%, while that of hepatitis C was 58.6%. Only 13.1% of participants had received hepatitis B vaccination. Screening results showed 2.8% tested positive for hepatitis B, with none positive for hepatitis C.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that while awareness of hepatitis B was relatively high, the low vaccination uptake remains a major concern, highlighting the need for improved health education and preventive measures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted health education programs during antenatal visits and emphasize the importance of regular screening and vaccination to reduce the burden of hepatitis B infections among pregnant women.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and small sample size, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

使用 Kshar Sutra 成功治疗乙型肝炎患者复发性肛瘘的 治疗:病例报告

Successful treatment of recurrent anal fistula in a patient with hepatitis B using Kshar Sutra: a case report
作者： Prasad Bapat, Vaidehi Bapat, Deepak Sadashiv Phalgune
摘要中文翻译尽管描述了几种 治疗,但治疗肛瘘的理想 治疗 仍然难以捉摸。肛瘘的 治疗 是在保持节制和预防复发之间走钢丝。患者男性,49岁,患有肛瘘9年。该患者的 HBsAg(澳大利亚抗原)呈阳性。治疗 使用了刹罗经线。没有给予伤口愈合或感染控制的内服药物。术后未出现感染、剧烈疼痛、严重出血、失禁等并发症。患者已完全痊愈,并已无病生存 33 个月。Kshara Sutra 是一种有效的 治疗,没有并发症并且完全治愈。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索 乙型肝炎病毒 感染患者复发性肛瘘的替代 治疗,因为治疗肛瘘的理想 治疗 仍然难以捉摸。
设计与对象
这篇病例报告描述了一名49岁男性患者的治疗,有肛瘘病史9年,HBsAg阳性,提示慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染。
核心方法
治疗 涉及使用刹罗经线,没有提供用于伤口愈合或感染控制的内服药物。
主要结果
患者完全康复,没有感染、剧烈疼痛、严重出血或失禁等并发症,并保持无病状态33个月。
解释与创新
作者认为,Kshara Sutra 是治疗 乙型肝炎病毒 感染患者复发性肛瘘的有效 治疗,未观察到并发症且完全愈合。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为 治疗 相关肛瘘患者制定替代 治疗 策略提供信息,特别是在传统 治疗 可能受到限制的情况下。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个 病例报告,可能无法推广,并且证据仅基于患者的临床结果。

Successful treatment of recurrent anal fistula in a patient with hepatitis B using Kshar Sutra: a case report

Authors: Prasad Bapat, Vaidehi Bapat, Deepak Sadashiv Phalgune
Original abstract (English)
The ideal treatment for fistula‐in‐ano remains elusive, despite several treatments being described. The treatment of fistula‐in‐ano is a tightrope walk between preserving continence and preventing recurrence. A 49-year-old male patient was suffering from fistula‐in‐ano for 9 years. The patient was positive for HBsAg (Australia antigen). Kshara Sutra thread was used for the treatment. No internal medication was given for wound healing or infection control. There were no complications such as infection, severe pain, severe bleeding, and incontinence after the procedure. The patient was completely cured and is disease-free for 33 months. Kshara Sutra is an effective treatment with no complications and complete healing.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore an alternative treatment for recurrent anal fistula in a patient with hepatitis B virus infection, as the ideal treatment for fistula-in-ano remains elusive.
Design and population
This case report describes the treatment of a 49-year-old male patient with a history of fistula-in-ano for 9 years and HBsAg positivity, indicating chronic hepatitis B virus infection.
Core methods
The treatment involved the use of Kshara Sutra thread, with no internal medication provided for wound healing or infection control.
Main results
The patient achieved complete healing with no complications such as infection, severe pain, severe bleeding, or incontinence, and remained disease-free for 33 months.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that Kshara Sutra is an effective treatment for recurrent anal fistula in patients with hepatitis B virus infection, with no complications and complete healing observed.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of alternative treatment strategies for patients with hepatitis B virus-associated anal fistula, particularly in settings where conventional treatments may be limited.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case report, which may not be generalizable, and the evidence is based solely on the patient's clinical outcome.

聚乙二醇化干扰素 α-2b 添加到核苷(酸)类似物可减少肝细胞癌和晚期纤维化的失代偿 慢性乙型肝炎

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B
作者： Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
摘要中文翻译背景目前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。然而,其在改善 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者预后方面的价值以及该联合策略的合适候选者仍有待进一步探索。目的 评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。方法 这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了诊断为 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化的患者。参与者被分为两组:(1)接受联合治疗(Peg-IFNα-2b 附加到正在进行的 NA 治疗);(2) 接受 NA 单一疗法的患者。采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。结果 该分析包括 302 对配对,中位随访时间为 220 周。与 NA 单一疗法相比,联合治疗与发生 HCC(风险比 = 0.22;95% CI:0.11-0.48)和失代偿性肝硬化(风险比 = 0.39;95% CI:0.20-0.77)的风险显着降低相关。这些益处在经验证的 HCC 预测模型分类为中高风险的患者以及持续生化异常持续 > 12 个月的患者中尤其明显。结论 Peg-IFNα-2b 与 NA 的附加治疗与患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者的 HCC 减少和失代偿显着相关,支持其在该人群中的使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估附加 Peg-IFNα-2b 治疗对 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化患者的长期预后益处。当前在核苷(酸)类似物(NA)中添加聚乙二醇化干扰素(Peg-IFN)α-2b的治疗策略主要旨在实现功能性治愈。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究纳入了诊断患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或代偿性肝硬化的患者。参与者被分为两组:(1)接受联合治疗(Peg-IFNα-2b 附加到正在进行的 NA 治疗);(2) 接受 NA 单一疗法的患者。
核心方法
采用 1:1 倾向评分匹配方法来平衡各组之间的基线特征。在生存分析中,肝细胞癌 (HCC) 和失代偿性肝硬化的结果根据基线临床特征和既定的 HCC 风险评分进行分层。
主要结果
该分析包括 302 对配对,中位随访时间为 220 周。与 NA 单一疗法相比,联合治疗与发生 HCC(风险比 = 0.22;95% CI:0.11-0.48)和失代偿性肝硬化(风险比 = 0.39;95% CI:0.20-0.77)的风险显着降低相关。
解释与创新
这些益处在经验证的 HCC 预测模型分类为中高风险的患者以及持续生化异常持续 > 12 个月的患者中尤其明显。Peg-IFNα-2b 与 NA 的附加治疗与患有 HBV 相关晚期纤维化或肝硬化的高危患者的 HCC 减少和代偿失调显着相关,支持其在该人群中的使用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Peg-IFNα-2b 联合治疗可能为减少高危 HBV 患者的 HCC 和失代偿带来重要益处。这可以为未来 治疗 治疗 慢性乙型肝炎 晚期纤维化和肝硬化的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性队列研究研究,可能会引入偏倚和混杂因素。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于倾向评分匹配和分层生存分析。

Pegylated interferon α-2b add-on to nucleos(t)ide analogues reduces hepatocellular carcinoma and decompensation in advanced fibrotic chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Xiang-Yong Li, Jing-Tao Huang, Ting Liu, Ying-Yi Li, Long Zhe, Hong Cao, Bing-Liang Lin
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure. However, its value in improving prognosis and the suitable candidates for this combination strategy in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis remain to be further explored. AIM To evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. METHODS This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy. A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores. RESULTS The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy. These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. CONCLUSION Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the long-term prognosis benefit of add-on Peg-IFNα-2b therapy in patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. The current therapeutic strategy of adding pegylated interferon (Peg-IFN) α-2b to nucleos(t)ide analogues (NAs) is primarily aimed at achieving functional cure.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study enrolled patients diagnosed with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or compensated cirrhosis. Participants were divided into two groups: (1) Those receiving combination therapy (Peg-IFNα-2b add-on to ongoing NA treatment); and (2) Those receiving NA monotherapy.
Core methods
A 1:1 propensity score matching approach was applied to balance baseline characteristics between groups. In survival analysis, the outcomes of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and decompensated cirrhosis were stratified by baseline clinical features and established HCC risk scores.
Main results
The analysis included 302 matched pairs, with a median follow-up duration of 220 weeks. Combination therapy was associated with a significantly lower risk of developing HCC (hazard ratio = 0.22; 95%CI: 0.11-0.48) and decompensated cirrhosis (hazard ratio = 0.39; 95%CI: 0.20-0.77) than NA monotherapy.
Interpretation and novelty
These benefits were particularly pronounced among patients classified as intermediate-to-high risk by validated HCC prediction models and in those with persistent biochemical abnormalities lasting > 12 months. Add-on therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b to NAs is significantly associated with reduced HCC and decompensation in high-risk patients with HBV-related advanced fibrosis or cirrhosis, supporting its use in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that combination therapy with Peg-IFNα-2b may offer important benefits for reducing HCC and decompensation in high-risk HBV patients. This could inform future treatment strategies for managing advanced fibrosis and cirrhosis in chronic hepatitis B.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective cohort study, which may introduce bias and confounding factors. The evidence is moderate as it relies on propensity score matching and stratified survival analysis.

国家疫苗接种项目前后慢性乙型肝炎儿童的临床特征和结局

Clinical characteristics and outcomes of children with chronic hepatitis B before and after the national vaccination program
作者： Sebahat Çam
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 传播 (HBV) 儿童的临床特征、传播 途径和结局,并比较国家 传播 计划之前和之后出生的患者。其基本原理是需要了解 疫苗接种 对儿童 HBV 流行病学 的影响。
设计与对象
一项回顾性队列研究研究纳入了2006年至2025年间诊断为慢性乙型肝炎的387名0-18岁儿童。根据出生年份将患者分为疫苗接种前(≤1997年出生)和疫苗接种后(≥1998年出生)组。
核心方法
分析人口统计学、临床和血清学数据,以评估 疫苗接种 治疗前和 疫苗接种 治疗后组之间的差异。研究设计包括对指定时间段内诊断病例进行回顾性 队列研究 分析。
主要结果
随着时间的推移,慢性乙肝确诊病例数显着下降,2006 年至 2015 年期间确诊为 375 例,而 2016 年至 2025 年期间为 12 例(p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者将诊断病例的下降解释为国家 疫苗接种 计划在减少儿童乙型肝炎病毒感染方面的有效性的证据。这一发现强调了该计划对 HBV 流行病学 的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为国家 疫苗接种 计划在预防儿童 HBV 感染方面的成功提供了重要见解,可以为未来的公共卫生战略和 监测 工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计而受到限制,可能会引入偏差和混杂因素。然而,考虑到病例数的明显下降和结果的统计显着性,证据是强有力的。

Clinical characteristics and outcomes of children with chronic hepatitis B before and after the national vaccination program

Authors: Sebahat Çam
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To evaluate the clinical characteristics, transmission routes, and outcomes of children with chronic hepatitis B (HBV) andcompare patients born before and after the national vaccination program.Patients and Methods: This retrospective cohort study included 387 children aged 0–18 years diagnosed with chronic HBV infectionbetween 2006 and 2025. Patients were divided into pre-vaccination (born ≤1997) and post-vaccination (born ≥1998) groups.Demographic, clinical, and serological data were analyzed.Results: The number of diagnosed cases declined markedly over time, with 375 cases identified between 2006–2015 compared with12 cases between 2016–2025 (p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the clinical characteristics, transmission routes, and outcomes of children with chronic hepatitis B (HBV) and compare patients born before and after the national vaccination program. The rationale is grounded in the need to understand the impact of vaccination on HBV epidemiology in children.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted involving 387 children aged 0–18 years diagnosed with chronic HBV infection between 2006 and 2025. Patients were categorized into pre-vaccination (born ≤1997) and post-vaccination (born ≥1998) groups based on their birth years.
Core methods
Demographic, clinical, and serological data were analyzed to assess the differences between the pre-vaccination and post-vaccination groups. The study design included a retrospective cohort analysis of diagnosed cases over a defined time period.
Main results
The number of diagnosed cases of chronic HBV declined markedly over time, with 375 cases identified between 2006–2015 compared with 12 cases between 2016–2025 (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the decline in diagnosed cases as evidence of the effectiveness of the national vaccination program in reducing HBV infection in children. This finding highlights the program's impact on HBV epidemiology.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide important insights into the success of the national vaccination program in preventing HBV infection in children, which can inform future public health strategies and surveillance efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, which may introduce biases and confounding factors. However, the evidence is strong given the clear decline in cases and the statistical significance of the results.

尼日利亚卡杜纳州孕妇乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎病毒感染的血清流行率及危险因素

Seroprevalence and Risk Factors of Hepatitis B and C Virus Infections among Pregnant Women in Kaduna State, Nigeria
作者： Sarah Nuhu Kase, Christy Chinyere Fredrick, Dennis Amaechi, Esther Luka Abdulkarim, Garba Ninani, Danladi Jonah, Haruna Danlami Sambo, Magdaline Joseph Kwaji, Jamila Ibrahim Suleiman, Theophilus Idahosa Ojemudia
摘要中文翻译背景:肝炎是由具有传染性的病毒引起的,并且感染可以从一个人传染给另一个人。估计孕妇中乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎病毒感染的 患病率 将有助于降低这些受试者的 死亡率 和发病率。方法:根据样本的可用性,从 Saminaka、Lere 和 Gure 的三家医院采集了总共三百 (300) 份血液样本。使用体外诊断试剂盒(试纸/试纸条)对血清样本进行分析,以检测乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)和丙型肝炎病毒抗体(抗HCV)。结果:试纸显示,18/330(6.00%)的孕妇乙肝病毒检测呈阳性,15/300(5.00%)的孕妇感染丙肝病毒。酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA) 试剂盒证实研究人群中 25/300 (8.33%) 的 HBV 和 21/300 (7.00%) 的 HCV 存在。结论:输血前需要对血液进行适当的筛查,因为大多数接受过输血的女性被发现受到感染,也需要对该疾病进行适当的宣传,因为许多参与者不知道其传播并导致危及生命的感染的能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在估计孕妇中乙型和丙型肝炎病毒感染的 患病率,以降低这些受试者的 死亡率 和发病率。
设计与对象
根据样本可用性,对尼日利亚卡杜纳州的 300 名孕妇进行了一项横断面研究,这些孕妇是从萨米纳卡、莱尔和古雷的三家医院收集的。
核心方法
使用体外诊断试剂盒(试纸/试纸)对血清样本进行分析,以检测乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)和丙型肝炎病毒抗体(抗-HCV)。使用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)试剂盒证实了结果。
主要结果
试纸显示,18/330 (6.00%) 的孕妇乙肝病毒检测呈阳性,15/300 (5.00%) 的孕妇感染丙肝病毒。ELISA 证实研究人群中 25/300 (8.33%) 的 HBV 和 21/300 (7.00%) 的 HCV 存在。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了卡杜纳州孕妇中乙型肝炎病毒和丙型肝炎病毒感染的显着性,强调需要开展有针对性的筛查和宣传计划。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为筛查和预防孕妇乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎感染的公共卫生策略提供参考,有助于改善孕产妇和胎儿的健康结果。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖单一诊断方法和潜在的误报而受到限制;然而,由于方法论和样本量明确,证据并不充分。

Seroprevalence and Risk Factors of Hepatitis B and C Virus Infections among Pregnant Women in Kaduna State, Nigeria

Authors: Sarah Nuhu Kase, Christy Chinyere Fredrick, Dennis Amaechi, Esther Luka Abdulkarim, Garba Ninani, Danladi Jonah, Haruna Danlami Sambo, Magdaline Joseph Kwaji, Jamila Ibrahim Suleiman, Theophilus Idahosa Ojemudia
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis is caused by Viruses that are all contagious and the infection can be transmitted from one person to another. Estimating the prevalence of hepatitis B and C viral infections among pregnant women will help reduce mortality and morbidity rates in these subjects. Methodology: A total of three hundred (300) blood samples were collected from three Hospitals in Saminaka, Lere and Gure based on the availability of samples. The sera samples were assayed using in-vitro diagnostic kit (dipsticks/strips) to detect the hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and antibodies to hepatitis C virus (anti-HCV). Results: Test strip revealed that 18/330 (6.00%) of pregnant women tested positive for HBV and 15/300 (5.00%) were infected with HCV. The Enzyme Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA) kit confirmed 25/300 (8.33%) of HBV and 21/300 (7.00%) of HCV in the study population.. Conclusion: There is need for proper screening of blood before transfusion as most women that had blood transfusion once were found to be infected also there is need for proper sensitization about the disease as many participants were not aware of its capacity to spread and cause life threatening infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to estimate the prevalence of hepatitis B and C viral infections among pregnant women to reduce mortality and morbidity rates in these subjects.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 300 pregnant women in Kaduna State, Nigeria, collected from three hospitals in Saminaka, Lere, and Gure based on sample availability.
Core methods
Blood sera samples were assayed using in-vitro diagnostic kits (dipsticks/strips) to detect hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and antibodies to hepatitis C virus (anti-HCV). The results were confirmed using an Enzyme Linked Immunosorbent Assay (ELISA) kit.
Main results
Test strips revealed that 18/330 (6.00%) of pregnant women tested positive for HBV and 15/300 (5.00%) were infected with HCV. ELISA confirmed 25/300 (8.33%) of HBV and 21/300 (7.00%) of HCV in the study population.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the significant prevalence of HBV and HCV infections among pregnant women in Kaduna State, emphasizing the need for targeted screening and awareness programs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform public health strategies for screening and prevention of hepatitis B and C infections in pregnant women, contributing to improved maternal and fetal health outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single diagnostic method and the potential for false positives; however, the evidence is moderate due to the clear methodology and sample size.

不同的免疫特征定义了与乙型肝炎病毒相关和与丁型肝炎病毒相关的肝细胞癌。

Distinct immune signatures define hepatitis D- versus hepatitis B-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.
作者： Simon Glimmann, Katja Deterding, Kilian Bock, Birgit Bremer, Anke R.M. Kraft, Markus Cornberg, Heiner H. Wedemeyer, Bernd Heinrich, Anika Wranke
摘要中文翻译背景:肝细胞癌(HCC)是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一。丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)是病毒性肝炎中最严重的形式。然而,与HDV相关的HCC的临床、病毒学和全身炎症特征仍不够明确。方法:本研究为单中心回顾性研究,纳入了162例因慢性乙型肝炎(HBV-HCC;n=108)或慢性丁型肝炎(HDV-HCC;n=54)导致的HCC患者。使用邻近扩展检测法(proximity extension assay)对HCC患者的血浆中92种可溶性炎症介质(SIMs)水平进行了定量分析,包括50例HBV-HCC、30例HDV-HCC以及匹配的非HCC对照组(HBV=19,HDV=17)。结果:在HCC诊断时,慢性乙型肝炎患者年龄较小,肝合成和解毒功能受损更严重,AST、CRP和白细胞水平较低。HDV-HCC肿瘤体积较小,门静脉浸润较少见,且更常符合米兰标准(所有p<0.01)。全身免疫特征分析显示两种病因的HCC具有不同的炎症模式。与HBV-HCC相比,HDV-HCC表现出更强的IL-10相关免疫调节特征,而HBV-HCC则表现出更显著的促炎特征,涉及IL-6和磷酸化及增殖相关信号。尽管存在这些差异,各组的无事件生存率没有显著差异。结论:HBV-HCC更常表现为晚期肿瘤阶段,而HDV-HCC则与更严重的潜在肝病但较小的肿瘤相关。重要的是,HBV相关和HDV相关的HCC表现出不同的全身炎症特征,这可能反映了两种病因之间系统性免疫调节的差异。这些发现可能对生物标志物的开发和未来针对病因的治疗策略具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肝细胞癌 (HCC) 是全球癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因。
设计与对象
在这项单中心回顾性研究中,我们确定了 162 名因慢性丁型肝炎 (HDV-HCC;n=54) 或 慢性乙型肝炎 (HBV-HCC;n=108) 导致的 HCC 患者。
核心方法
使用邻近延伸测定对 HCC 患者 [HBV-HCC (n=50)、HDV-HCC (n=30) 和匹配的非 HCC 对照 (HBV=19、HDV=17)] 的 92 种可溶性炎症介质 (SIM) 血浆水平进行定量。
主要结果
与 HBV-HCC 相比,HDV-HCC 的特点是具有相对较强的 IL-10 相关免疫调节特征,而 HBV-HCC 表现出更明显的促炎特征,涉及 IL-6 以及磷酸化和增殖相关信号传导。
解释与创新
HBV-HCC 更常见于晚期肿瘤阶段,而 HDV-HCC 与更严重的潜在肝病但肿瘤更小相关。
科研与公卫意义
重要的是,HBV 相关和 HDV 相关 HCC 表现出不同的全身炎症特征,可能反映了病因之间全身免疫调节的差异。
局限与证据强度
这些发现可能对生物标志物的开发和未来的病因特异性治疗策略产生影响。

Distinct immune signatures define hepatitis D- versus hepatitis B-associated hepatocellular carcinoma.

Authors: Simon Glimmann, Katja Deterding, Kilian Bock, Birgit Bremer, Anke R.M. Kraft, Markus Cornberg, Heiner H. Wedemeyer, Bernd Heinrich, Anika Wranke
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide. Hepatitis D is the most severe form of viral hepatitis. However, the clinical, virological, and systemic inflammatory characteristics of HDV-associated HCC remain insufficiently characterized. METHODS: In this single-center retrospective study, we identified 162 patients with HCC due to chronic hepatitis D (HDV-HCC; n=54) or chronic hepatitis B (HBV-HCC; n=108). Plasma levels of 92 soluble inflammatory mediators (SIMs) were quantified using proximity extension assay in HCC patients [HBV-HCC (n=50), HDV-HCC (n=30), and matched non-HCC controls (HBV=19, HDV=17)]. RESULTS: At the time of HCC diagnosis, patients with CHD were younger, had more impaired liver synthesis and detoxification function, and lower levels of AST, CRP, and leukocytes. HDV-HCCs presented with smaller tumors, less frequent portal vein infiltration, and more often fulfilled Milan criteria (all p<0.01). Systemic immune profiling revealed distinct inflammatory patterns between both etiologies. Compared with HBV-HCC, HDV-HCC was characterized by a comparatively stronger IL-10-associated immunoregulatory signature, whereas HBV-HCC exhibited a more pronounced pro-inflammatory profile involving IL-6- and phosphorylation- and proliferation-associated signaling. Despite these differences, event-free survival did not differ between groups. CONCLUSIONS: HBV-HCC more frequently presented with advanced tumor stage, whereas HDV-HCC was associated with more severe underlying liver disease but smaller tumors. Importantly, HBV-associated and HDV-associated HCC exhibit distinct systemic inflammatory signatures, potentially reflecting differences in systemic immune regulation between etiologies. These findings may have implications for biomarker development and future etiology-specific therapeutic strategies.

Question and background
Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide.
Design and population

Core methods

Main results
Compared with HBV-HCC, HDV-HCC was characterized by a comparatively stronger IL-10-associated immunoregulatory signature, whereas HBV-HCC exhibited a more pronounced pro-inflammatory profile involving IL-6- and phosphorylation- and proliferation-associated signaling.
Interpretation and novelty
HBV-HCC more frequently presented with advanced tumor stage, whereas HDV-HCC was associated with more severe underlying liver disease but smaller tumors.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

妊娠中晚期低病毒血症乙型肝炎病毒脱氧核糖核酸升高的危险因素及预测模型

Risk factors and predictive model for hepatitis B virus deoxyribonucleic acid elevation in mid-to-late pregnancy with low viremia
作者： Zi‐Ren Chen, Hui‐Hui Chen, Bing‐Wang Jie, Ting‐Ting Peng, Ying Lin, Ya‐Wen Geng, Yu‐Wei Shen, Yue‐Ying Deng, Dan Xie, Li‐Li Yang

Risk factors and predictive model for hepatitis B virus deoxyribonucleic acid elevation in mid-to-late pregnancy with low viremia

Authors: Zi‐Ren Chen, Hui‐Hui Chen, Bing‐Wang Jie, Ting‐Ting Peng, Ying Lin, Ya‐Wen Geng, Yu‐Wei Shen, Yue‐Ying Deng, Dan Xie, Li‐Li Yang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Managing pregnant women with a low hepatitis B virus (HBV) viral load (HBV DNA AIM To identify the risk factors for HBV DNA elevation during pregnancy and to develop a predictive model to guide individualized management and reduce mother-to-child transmission (MTCT) risk. METHODS This retrospective multicenter control study included 430 pregnant women with low HBV DNA loads ( RESULTS The elevated HBV DNA group had a higher maternal age (P = 0.008), hepatitis B virus e antigen positivity, and baseline HBV DNA level ≥ 100 IU/mL (P 1 × 104 IU/mL reached high-risk levels. CONCLUSION Maternal age and baseline HBV DNA level of ≥ 100 IU/mL were identified as critical predictors of viral elevation during pregnancy. The predictive model offers moderate predictive accuracy and clinical utility, supporting tailored surveillance for low viral load chronic hepatitis B pregnancies to further reduce MTCT risk.

埃塞俄比亚人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型肝炎病毒 和丙型肝炎病毒控制和消除政策、法规和策略的挑战和机遇

Challenges and Opportunities of Human Immunodeficiency Virus, Hepatitis B Virus and Hepatitis C Virus Control and Elimination Policies, Regulations, and Strategies in Ethiopia
作者： Dagnaw M, Geremew B, Gezie L
摘要中文翻译了解利益相关者的经历和观点对于识别政策意识、参与、实施和项目协调中的差距至关重要。目标:本研究旨在探讨影响埃塞俄比亚艾滋病病毒(HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)控制和消除政策、法规和战略的发展、实施和效果的挑战和机遇。方法:本研究采用定性研究方法,在埃塞俄比亚参与艾滋病病毒(HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)政策制定、实施、监管、监测、研究和服务提供的利益相关者中进行,基于机构和设施。数据于2023年4月17日至5月27日之间通过定性访谈和焦点小组讨论收集,同时结合相关政策、战略、指南和项目文件的审查以及选定的服务提供和药品供应环境的观察。几乎所有参与者,32人(94.1%),都指出了影响政策和战略实施的挑战,包括与新冠状病毒(COVID-19)相关的中断以及将政策和战略转化为实践的担忧。结论:研究结果表明,埃塞俄比亚在艾滋病病毒(HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)控制和消除政策、法规和战略的职责认知与实际意识和实施之间存在显著差距。摘要:背景:艾滋病病毒(HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)仍然是全球和埃塞俄比亚的主要公共卫生挑战。尽管已经制定了支持其控制和消除的政策、法规和战略,但将这些政策承诺转化为有效的实施仍然面临挑战。根据专业角色,有34名参与者被有意识地选择参与研究...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估埃塞俄比亚控制和消除艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎政策的挑战和机遇,强调政策认识与实际执行之间的差距。
设计与对象
该研究于 2023 年 4 月 17 日至 5 月 27 日在埃塞俄比亚进行,涉及 34 名特意挑选的参与者,他们代表艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎政策制定、实施和服务提供的主要利益相关者。
核心方法
通过定性访谈、焦点小组讨论、政策、战略、指南和项目文件审查以及卫生服务和制药机构的观察来收集数据。
主要结果
只有 35.3% 的参与者清楚地认识到 HIV、HBV 和 HCV 控制政策的存在及其主要特征,而 94.1% 的参与者了解利益相关者的角色,但只有 23.5% 的参与者报告了明确的实施情况。
解释与创新
调查结果表明,政策认知与实际实施之间存在巨大差距,大多数参与者都指出了诸如 COVID-19 干扰和缺乏将政策转化为实践等挑战。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调,埃塞俄比亚需要改进政策实施和利益相关者参与,以有效控制和消除艾滋病毒、乙肝病毒和丙肝病毒。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其定性性质和有目的的抽样而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。由于关注的是利益相关者的观点而不是定量结果,证据是温和的。

Challenges and Opportunities of Human Immunodeficiency Virus, Hepatitis B Virus and Hepatitis C Virus Control and Elimination Policies, Regulations, and Strategies in Ethiopia

Authors: Dagnaw M, Geremew B, Gezie L
Original abstract (English)
Understanding stakeholder experiences and perspectives is important for identifying gaps in policy awareness, participation, implementation, and program coordination.ObjectiveThis study aimed to explore the challenges and opportunities affecting the development, implementation, and effectiveness of HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination policies, regulations, and strategies in Ethiopia.MethodsA facility‐ and institution‐based qualitative study was conducted among stakeholders involved in HIV, HBV, and HCV policy development, implementation, regulation, monitoring, research, and service delivery. Data were collected from April 17 to May 27, 2023, using qualitative interviews and focus group discussions, complemented by a review of relevant policy, strategy, guideline, and program documents and observations of selected service‐delivery and pharmaceutical settings. Nearly all participants, 32 (94.1%), identified challenges affecting policy and strategy implementation, including COVID‐19‐related disruptions and concerns regarding the translation of policies and strategies into practice.ConclusionThe findings indicate a substantial gap between recognition of stakeholder responsibilities and the practical awareness and implementation of HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination policies, regulations, and strategies in Ethiopia. ABSTRACTBackgroundHuman immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), and hepatitis C virus (HCV) remain major public health challenges globally and in Ethiopia. Although policies, regulations, and strategies have been developed to support their control and elimination, translating these policy commitments into effective implementation remains challenging. Thirty‐four participants were purposively selected based on their professional roles...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the challenges and opportunities in the control and elimination of HIV, HBV, and HCV policies in Ethiopia, highlighting the gap between policy recognition and practical implementation.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Ethiopia from April 17 to May 27, 2023, and involved 34 purposively selected participants representing key stakeholders in HIV, HBV, and HCV policy formulation, implementation, and service delivery.
Core methods
Data were collected through qualitative interviews, focus group discussions, and a review of policy, strategy, guideline, and program documents, alongside observations from health-service and pharmaceutical settings.
Main results
Only 35.3% of participants clearly identified the existence and key features of HIV, HBV, and HCV control policies, while 94.1% understood stakeholder roles, but only 23.5% reported clear implementation status.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate a significant gap between policy recognition and practical implementation, with most participants identifying challenges such as COVID-19 disruptions and lack of translation of policies into practice.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the need for improved policy implementation and stakeholder engagement in Ethiopia to effectively control and eliminate HIV, HBV, and HCV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its qualitative nature and purposive sampling, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the focus on stakeholder perspectives rather than quantitative outcomes.
