华支睾吸虫共感染增强 慢性乙型肝炎 患者多形核骨髓源性抑制细胞的扩增和代谢重编程

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients
作者： Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
摘要中文翻译背景已知华支睾吸虫(C. sinensis)合并感染会加速乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在两种感染流行的地区。代谢重编程已成为免疫抑制的关键驱动因素。这些代谢变化通常是由骨髓源性抑制细胞(MDSC)精心策划的,这些细胞在慢性感染期间会扩张以促进免疫耐受。中华丝虫合并感染会重新编程代谢以有利于 MDSC 相关的抑制,而这一过程与正常肝酶水平的关系仍不清楚。目的 探讨中华支原体合并感染是否会诱导肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者发生 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。方法 在这项横断面研究中,总共招募了 117 名参与者,并分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、单一感染中华支原体的参与者(n = 29)、单一感染 HBV 的参与者(n = 28)和混合感染的参与者(n = 30)。使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。进行 Spearman 相关性和中介分析来探讨炎症、代谢和免疫之间的关系。结果 HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,与HBV单一感染组相比,合并感染组的炎症细胞因子白细胞介素6(IL-6)和肿瘤坏死因子-α(TNF-α)水平较高,同时干扰素-γ(IFN-γ)水平明显较低。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。在代谢方面,共同感染组的鸟氨酸与精氨酸的比率显着较高。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。中华线虫合并感染通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高与免疫抑制相关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨华支睾吸虫合并感染是否会在肝酶水平正常的 HBV 患者中诱导 MDSC 相关的代谢重编程。背景强调,已知中华支原体合并感染会加速乙型肝炎患者的疾病进展并诱导免疫耐受,特别是在流行地区。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究纳入了 117 名参与者,分为四组:健康对照(n = 30)、中华支原体单一感染参与者(n = 29)、HBV 单一感染参与者(n = 28)和混合感染参与者(n = 30)。
核心方法
使用化学发光免疫测定法测量血清细胞因子谱,使用液相色谱-串联质谱法测量氨基酸代谢物,并使用流式细胞术测量MDSC子集。流式细胞术检测显示,多形核MDSC(PMN-MDSC)在共感染组中表现出最大的扩增,并且其比例与IL-6和TNF-α水平密切相关。
主要结果
HBV单一感染和合并感染患者的肝酶和HBV DNA水平无统计学差异。然而,共同感染组的炎症细胞因子:白细胞介素-6 (IL-6) 和肿瘤坏死因子-α (TNF-α) 水平较高,同时干扰素-γ (IFN-γ) 水平明显较低。中介分析表明,PMN-MDSC 约占 IL-6 对 IFN-γ 抑制总效应的 45%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,中华线虫合并感染与通过 IL-6/TNF-α 连接的 PMN-MDSC 扩增和鸟氨酸与精氨酸比率升高而产生的免疫抑制有关,导致尽管肝酶水平正常,但 IFN-γ 的产生受到抑制。这一发现强调了共感染可能调节 HBV 患者 免疫应答 的新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于了解合并感染 HBV 患者的免疫调节,并可能指导未来基于免疫的治疗和针对合并感染情况的 监测 策略的研究。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了其建立因果关系的能力。证据基于相关性和中介分析,可能无法完全捕捉感染与 免疫应答 之间复杂的相互作用。信心是中等的。

Clonorchis sinensis co-infection potentiates polymorphonuclear-myeloid-derived suppressor cells expansion and metabolic reprogramming in chronic hepatitis B patients

Authors: Jie-Ru Qiu, Hong-Bin Zhang, Mei Shang, Jue Xu, Yue-Chun Fu, Yuan Liao, Bo Hu, Hui-Min Dong
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Clonorchis sinensis (C. sinensis ) co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV), particularly in regions where both infections are endemic. Metabolic reprogramming has emerged as a key driver of immunosuppression. These metabolic shifts are often orchestrated by myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs), which expand during chronic infections to promote immune tolerance. sinensis co-infection reprograms metabolism to favor MDSC-linked suppression and how this process relates to normal liver enzyme levels remains unclear. AIM To investigate whether C. sinensis co-infection induces MDSC- linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. METHODS In this cross-sectional study, a total of 117 participants were enrolled and assigned to four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30). Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Spearman correlation and mediation analyses were performed to explore the relationships among inflammation, metabolism, and immunity. RESULTS HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, compared with the HBV mono-infected group, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels. Metabolically, the co-infected group had a significantly higher ornithine-to-arginine ratio. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether Clonorchis sinensis co-infection induces MDSC-linked metabolic reprogramming in patients with HBV and normal liver enzyme levels. The background highlights that C. sinensis co-infection is known to accelerate disease progression and induce immune tolerance in HBV patients, particularly in endemic regions.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study enrolled 117 participants divided into four groups: Healthy controls (n = 30), C. sinensis mono-infected participants (n = 29), HBV mono-infected participants (n = 28), and co-infected participants (n = 30).
Core methods
Serum cytokine profiles were measured using chemiluminescence immunoassay, amino acid metabolites using liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry, and MDSC subsets using flow cytometry. Flow cytometric assay revealed that polymorphonuclear MDSCs (PMN-MDSCs) showed the greatest expansion in the co-infected group, and their proportion correlated strongly with IL-6 and TNF-α levels.
Main results
HBV mono-infected and co-infected patients showed no statistically significant differences in liver enzyme and HBV DNA levels. However, the co-infected group had higher levels of the inflammatory cytokine: Interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α), accompanied by markedly lower interferon-γ (IFN-γ) levels. Mediation analysis suggested that PMN-MDSCs accounted for approximately 45% of the total effect of IL-6 on IFN-γ suppression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that C. sinensis co-infection was associated with immunosuppression through IL-6/TNF-α-linked PMN-MDSC expansion and elevated ornithine-to-arginine ratio, resulting in suppressed IFN-γ production despite normal liver enzyme levels. This finding highlights a novel mechanism by which co-infection may modulate immune responses in HBV patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the understanding of immune modulation in HBV patients with co-infections, potentially guiding future research on immune-based therapies and surveillance strategies for co-infection scenarios.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, limiting its ability to establish causality. The evidence is based on correlations and mediation analyses, which may not fully capture the complex interactions between infections and immune responses. The confidence is moderate.

乙型肝炎病毒 插入突变与肝细胞癌:机制和临床意义

Hepatitis B Virus Insertional Mutagenesis and Hepatocellular Carcinoma: Mechanisms and Clinical Significance
作者： Bianca Thakkar, George Y. Wu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)插入突变导致肝细胞癌(HCC)的机制及其临床意义。这一背景强调了 HBV 整合在肿瘤发生中的作用以及了解其对肿瘤生物学影响的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究分析了非肿瘤和肿瘤肝组织中的 HBV 整合模式,重点关注整合位点的克隆性和多样性。它检查了 HBV 相关 HCC 患者,并将其与非 HBV 相关 HCC 病例进行比较,以评估整合事件与肿瘤抑制基因改变之间的关联。
核心方法
基因组 全测序用于识别 HCC 中的复发性整合热点,包括 TERT、KMT2B/MLL4 和 CCNE1。该研究还通过分析 HBx 蛋白的断点和截短,评估了 HBV 整合对肿瘤抑制途径的频率和影响。
主要结果
在高达 79.3% 的 HBV 相关 HCC 病例中检测到 C 端截短的 HBx 蛋白,并且与转移潜力的特征相关。在高达 48% 的 HBV 相关肿瘤中发现了肿瘤抑制基因的改变,包括 突变 和缺失,而在非 HBV 相关的 HCC 中这一比例为 16%。HBV 整合事件与 244 个肿瘤抑制基因的缺失或失活相关,其中包括 TP53 和 RB1。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HBV 整合通过直接破坏肿瘤抑制基因和增强子介导的癌基因激活而导致肝癌发生。该研究强调了整合相关的结构重排在促进侵袭性肿瘤生物学方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于改善 HCC 中 HBV 驱动的致癌途径的检测和监测具有重要意义。他们还强调需要进行整合位点分析,以更好地了解 HBV 相关肝癌发生的机制并指导临床决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于观察数据,并未建立因果关系。证据是中等的,因为它基于基因组分析和临床观察,没有对 HBV 整合与 HCC 进展之间的因果关系进行实验验证。

Hepatitis B Virus Insertional Mutagenesis and Hepatocellular Carcinoma: Mechanisms and Clinical Significance

Authors: Bianca Thakkar, George Y. Wu
Original abstract (English)
2).10 Ectopic MYC overexpression driven by HBV-Enh I integration has been reported to promote HCC cell migration and invasion.10 A preferential breakpoint at the 3′ end of hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx) results in C-terminally truncated HBx variants with enhanced oncogenic potential.9 These 3′-deleted proteins lack regulatory domains involved in p53-dependent transcription, Sp1 binding, and growth suppression, thereby promoting cellular proliferation, transformation, and invasion.9 C-terminally truncated HBx was detected in up to 79.3% of HBV-associated HCC cases and was associated with venous invasion, increased c-Jun activity, metalloproteinase expression, mitogen-activated protein kinase activation, and cell-cycle dysregulation—features of metastatic potential.9 The generation of C-terminally truncated HBx proteins is supported by experimental and clinical studies, although its relative contribution to human hepatocarcinogenesis remains under investigation. Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global health burden, with an estimated 254 million people living with chronic infection worldwide in 2022.1 Chronic HBV infection is a leading cause of cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), accounting for more than half of HCC cases globally.2 Despite vaccination and antiviral therapy, HBV-related HCC continues to contribute substantially to cancer-related mortality.3,4 Importantly, HBV infection confers a risk of HCC even in the absence of cirrhosis, suggesting direct virus-associated genomic mechanisms beyond inflammation and fibrosis.5 Although HBV lacks a viral oncogene, it can promote tumor development through genetic damage, chromosomal instability, and the expression of viral regulatory elements.3,6 HBV replicates through reverse transcription o...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the mechanisms by which hepatitis B virus (HBV) insertional mutagenesis contributes to hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and its clinical significance. The background highlights the role of HBV integration in oncogenesis and the need to understand its impact on tumor biology.
Design and population
The study analyzed HBV integration patterns in both nontumor and tumor liver tissues, focusing on the clonality and diversity of integration sites. It examined patients with HBV-related HCC and compared them with non-HBV-related HCC cases to assess the association between integration events and tumor suppressor gene alterations.
Core methods
Genome-wide sequencing was used to identify recurrent integration hotspots in HCC, including TERT, KMT2B/MLL4, and CCNE1. The study also evaluated the frequency and impact of HBV integration on tumor suppressor pathways through analysis of breakpoints and truncation of HBx proteins.
Main results
C-terminally truncated HBx proteins were detected in up to 79.3% of HBV-associated HCC cases and were linked to features of metastatic potential. Tumor suppressor alterations, including mutations and deletions, were found in up to 48% of HBV-related tumors, compared with 16% of non-HBV-related HCCs. HBV integration events were associated with the deletion or inactivation of 244 tumor suppressor genes, including TP53 and RB1.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HBV integration contributes to hepatocarcinogenesis through both direct disruption of tumor suppressor genes and enhancer-mediated oncogene activation. The study highlights the importance of integration-associated structural rearrangements in promoting aggressive tumor biology.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have implications for improving the detection and monitoring of HBV-driven oncogenic pathways in HCC. They also emphasize the need for integration-site analysis to better understand the mechanisms of HBV-related hepatocarcinogenesis and guide clinical decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on observational data and does not establish causality. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on genomic analysis and clinical observations without experimental validation of the causal relationship between HBV integration and HCC progression.

全球抗病毒药物疗法在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌中的应用不足及其生存结局:一项纳入78项研究和68,243例患者的系统综述和荟萃分析。

Global Underutilization of Antiviral Therapy and Survival Outcomes in Hepatitis B Virus-Related Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of 78 Studies and 68,243 Patients.
作者： Sahith Kudaravalli, Taotao Yan, Cheng-Hao Tseng, Yao-Chun Hsu, Xiaohe Li, Yuankai Wu, Bowu Chen, Xiangrong Gao, Sicheng Gao, Danfeng Ren
摘要中文翻译使用核苷(酸)类似物进行抗病毒药物治疗被推荐用于所有具有病毒血症的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝硬化的患者,无论其病毒载量或肝酶水平如何。然而,肝细胞癌(HCC)并不是美国HBV 治疗指南中的治疗标准。尽管之前的研究报告指出,在HBV相关HCC患者中,抗病毒药物治疗与改善生存率相关,但目前仍缺乏明确的指南。为了指导指南制定,我们估计了抗病毒药物治疗率及其与生存率和HCC复发率之间的关联。我们按照PRISMA指南,对2006年至2024年间发表的、报告了HBV相关HCC成人患者抗病毒药物治疗率、生存率和/或HCC复发率的研究进行了系统综述和荟萃分析。使用随机效应模型汇总研究估计值。初始共有4556项研究,其中178项进行了全文审查,最终分析了78项(共68,243名患者)。汇总的抗病毒药物治疗率(52项研究,41,345名患者)为53.1%。在报告了HCC前抗病毒药物治疗启动的研究中,17.2%的HBV相关HCC患者在HCC发生前接受了治疗。接受治疗(与未接受治疗相比)的患者3年和5年总生存率(OS)更高(80.4% vs. 46.8%,两者p < 0.01)。在非活动性HCC研究中,5年OS为78.0% vs. 56.4%(p < 0.01),而在活动性HCC研究中,5年OS为48.2% vs. 22.6%(p = 0.14)。在多变量荟萃回归分析中,抗病毒药物治疗仍与改善5年OS独立相关。HCC复发率没有显著差异。抗病毒药物治疗与改善长期生存率相关,但在HBV相关HCC中仍被低估使用。这些发现突显了护理中的关键差距,并强调了在HBV相关HCC患者中更早和更一致地启动抗病毒药物治疗的必要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 抗病毒药物 疗法在 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患者中的全球利用不足及其对生存结果的影响。核心问题是 抗病毒药物 疗法是否可以提高 HBV 相关 HCC 的生存率以及其在不同人群中的使用情况如何比较。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 2006 年至 2024 年间发表的涉及 68,243 名患者的 78 项研究进行的系统分析和荟萃分析。这些研究是根据 PRISMA 指南选择的,并使用随机效应模型来汇总研究估计值。该综述纳入了报告 抗病毒药物 治疗、患有 HBV 相关 HCC 的成人患者的生存率和/或 HCC 复发率的研究。
共识与结论
抗病毒药物 核苷(酸)类似物治疗可改善 HBV 相关 HCC 的长期生存率。与未经治疗的患者相比,接受治疗的患者具有更高的 3 年和 5 年总生存 (OS) 率。然而,抗病毒药物 疗法目前在美国还不是 HCC 的 治疗 标准,其在临床实践中的使用情况仍然报道不足。
争议与缺口
接受治疗和未接受治疗的患者之间的 HCC 复发率没有显着差异。此外,该审查强调了护理方面的一个关键差距,因为 抗病毒药物 疗法尽管具有潜在的益处,但尚未得到充分利用。证据还表明,抗病毒药物 治疗与生存之间的关联可能因 HCC 的活动性而异。
科研与实践启示
为了改善患者的治疗效果,HBV 相关 HCC 患者需要更早、更一致地开始 抗病毒药物。研究结果强调了修订现行 治疗 指南的重要性,将 抗病毒药物 疗法纳入治疗 HBV 相关 HCC 的标准,特别是在目前尚未充分利用的人群中。

Global Underutilization of Antiviral Therapy and Survival Outcomes in Hepatitis B Virus-Related Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis of 78 Studies and 68,243 Patients.

Authors: Sahith Kudaravalli, Taotao Yan, Cheng-Hao Tseng, Yao-Chun Hsu, Xiaohe Li, Yuankai Wu, Bowu Chen, Xiangrong Gao, Sicheng Gao, Danfeng Ren
Original abstract (English)
Antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogues is recommended for patients with viremic hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related cirrhosis regardless of levels of viremia or liver enzymes. However, hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is not an antiviral treatment criterion in the U.S. HBV treatment guidelines, though prior studies have reported improved survival with antiviral treatment in HBV-related HCC. To inform guidelines, we estimated antiviral treatment rates and their association with survival and HCC recurrence rates in HBV-related HCC. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis of studies published between 2006 and 2024 that reported antiviral treatment, survival, and/or HCC recurrence rates in adults with HBV-related HCC, following PRISMA guidelines. Random-effects models pooled study estimates. Of the initial 4556 studies, 178 underwent full-text review, and 78 (68,243 patients) were analysed. The pooled antiviral treatment rate (52 studies, 41,345 patients) was 53.1%. In studies reporting pre-HCC antiviral initiation, 17.2% of HBV-related HCC patients received therapy before diagnosis. Treated (vs untreated) patients had higher 3- and 5-year OS (80.4% vs. 46.8%, both p < 0.01). Five-year OS was 78.0% vs. 56.4% in inactive HCC (p < 0.01) and 48.2% vs. 22.6% in active HCC (p = 0.14). Antiviral therapy remained independently associated with improved 5-year OS in multivariable meta-regression. There was no significant difference in HCC recurrence rates. Antiviral therapy is associated with improved long-term survival but remains underutilized in HBV-related HCC. These findings highlight a critical gap in care and underscore the need for earlier and more consistent antiviral initiation in patients with HBV-related HCC.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the global underutilization of antiviral therapy in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and its impact on survival outcomes. The central question is whether antiviral therapy improves survival in HBV-related HCC and how its utilization compares across different populations.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis and meta-analysis of 78 studies involving 68,243 patients, published between 2006 and 2024. The studies were selected following PRISMA guidelines, and random-effects models were used to pool study estimates. The review included studies that reported antiviral treatment, survival, and/or HCC recurrence rates in adults with HBV-related HCC.
Consensus and conclusions
Antiviral therapy with nucleos(t)ide analogues is associated with improved long-term survival in HBV-related HCC. Treated patients had higher 3- and 5-year overall survival (OS) rates compared to untreated patients. However, antiviral therapy is not currently a treatment criterion for HCC in the U.S., and its utilization remains underreported in clinical practice.
Controversies and gaps
There is no significant difference in HCC recurrence rates between treated and untreated patients. Additionally, the review highlights a critical gap in care, as antiviral therapy is underutilized despite its potential benefits. The evidence also suggests that the association between antiviral therapy and survival may vary depending on the activity of HCC.
Research and practice implications
To improve patient outcomes, there is a need for earlier and more consistent antiviral initiation in patients with HBV-related HCC. The findings emphasize the importance of revising current treatment guidelines to include antiviral therapy as a criterion for managing HBV-related HCC, particularly in populations where it is currently underutilized.

HBeAg阴性慢性乙型肝炎中有限与无限核苷(酸)类似物治疗的对比:随机试验的荟萃分析

Finite Versus Indefinite Nucleos(t)ide Analogue Therapy in HBeAg‐Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Meta‐Analysis of Randomized Trials.
作者： Demver P Gomez, Wilmyr F Hababag
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究探讨有限疗程与连续疗程核苷(酸)类似物(NA)治疗在HBeAg阴性慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)成人中的疗效和安全性。核心问题是有限疗程是否能增加HBsAg丢失的可能性,同时维持病毒抑制。
证据基础与方法
本综述纳入了四项平行组随机对照试验(RCTs),比较非肝硬化、病毒抑制的HBeAg阴性成人中NA停药与继续治疗的效果。两名评审员独立筛选研究并使用Cochrane RoB 2评估偏倚风险。采用随机效应Mantel-Haenszel荟萃分析,结合Hartung-Knapp调整和Paule-Mandel估计方法,合并风险比(RRs)及其95%置信区间(CIs)。证据的确定性通过GRADE方法进行评估。
共识与结论
与连续治疗相比,有限疗程的NA治疗可能增加HBsAg丢失的概率,尽管证据确定性较低。研究发现,停药与HBsAg丢失的风险比为5.02(95% CI:0.47-53.47),相当于每1000名患者约有53例额外的HBsAg丢失。然而,有限疗程显著增加了病毒学复发(RR 23.09)和再次治疗(RR 21.94)的风险,同时降低了持续停药后的病毒学应答率(RR 0.46)。未报告肝功能失代偿或严重不良事件。
争议与缺口
国际指南对有限疗程与连续疗程NA治疗的推荐存在分歧。关于HBsAg丢失的证据确定性较低,且置信区间较宽,表明效果估计存在不确定性。此外,本研究未涉及长期结局或有限疗程对乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝细胞癌或肝硬化的潜在影响。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究以明确有限疗程NA治疗的长期安全性和有效性,特别是其对HBV相关肝细胞癌和肝硬化的潜在影响。研究结果表明,有限疗程可能在专家中心作为实现功能性治愈的一种选择性策略,但需要更多数据支持其更广泛的应用。

Finite Versus Indefinite Nucleos(t)ide Analogue Therapy in HBeAg‐Negative Chronic Hepatitis B: Meta‐Analysis of Randomized Trials.

Authors: Demver P Gomez, Wilmyr F Hababag
Original abstract (English)
Continuous nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy for chronic hepatitis B (CHB) effectively maintains viral suppression but rarely results in functional cure. Finite NA therapy has been proposed to increase rates of HBsAg loss; however, international guidelines provide conflicting recommendations. This study compares the efficacy and safety of finite versus continuous NA therapy in HBeAg-negative adults using randomized controlled trials. We included parallel-group randomized controlled trials (RCTs) comparing nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) cessation versus continuation in non-cirrhotic, virally suppressed HBeAg-negative adults. Two reviewers independently screened studies and assessed risk of bias using Cochrane RoB 2. Random-effects Mantel-Haenszel meta-analyses with Hartung-Knapp adjustment and Paule-Mandel estimation were used to pool risk ratios (RRs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Certainty of evidence was assessed using the GRADE approach. Four RCTs (n = 357) met inclusion criteria. Discontinuation increased HBsAg loss vs. continuation (RR 5.02, 95% CI: 0.47-53.47; I 2 = 21.8%; low certainty), corresponding to ~53 additional losses per 1000 patients. However, finite therapy markedly increased virologic relapse (RR 23.09) and retreatment (RR 21.94) while reducing sustained off-therapy virologic response (RR 0.46). Post-cessation ALT flares were frequent but self-limited or resolved with retreatment. No hepatic decompensation or serious adverse events occurred. In monitored, non-cirrhotic adults, finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss despite predictable relapse and retreatment. With no evidence of severe harm, these findings support finite therapy as a selective, patient-centered strategy toward functional cure in expert centers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study investigates the efficacy and safety of finite versus continuous nucleos(t)ide analogue (NA) therapy in HBeAg-negative adults with chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The central question is whether finite therapy increases the likelihood of HBsAg loss while maintaining viral suppression.
Evidence base and method
The review includes four parallel-group randomized controlled trials (RCTs) comparing NA cessation versus continuation in non-cirrhotic, virally suppressed HBeAg-negative adults. Two reviewers independently screened studies and assessed risk of bias using Cochrane RoB 2. Random-effects Mantel-Haenszel meta-analyses with Hartung-Knapp adjustment and Paule-Mandel estimation were used to pool risk ratios (RRs) with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Certainty of evidence was evaluated using the GRADE approach.
Consensus and conclusions
Finite NA therapy may increase the probability of HBsAg loss compared to continuous therapy, although the evidence is of low certainty. The study found that discontinuation of therapy was associated with a risk ratio of 5.02 (95% CI: 0.47-53.47) for HBsAg loss, corresponding to approximately 53 additional losses per 1000 patients. However, finite therapy significantly increased virologic relapse (RR 23.09) and retreatment (RR 21.94) while reducing sustained off-therapy virologic response (RR 0.46). No hepatic decompensation or serious adverse events were reported.
Controversies and gaps
International guidelines provide conflicting recommendations on finite versus continuous NA therapy. The evidence for HBsAg loss is of low certainty, and the wide confidence interval suggests uncertainty in the effect estimate. Additionally, the study does not address long-term outcomes or the impact of finite therapy on HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma or cirrhosis.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to clarify the long-term safety and efficacy of finite NA therapy, particularly in relation to HBV-associated hepatocellular carcinoma and cirrhosis. The findings suggest that finite therapy could be considered as a selective strategy in expert centers for achieving functional cure, but more data are required to support its broader implementation.

埃塞俄比亚人类免疫缺陷病毒、乙型肝炎病毒 和丙型肝炎病毒控制和消除政策、法规和战略的挑战和机遇。

Challenges and Opportunities of Human Immunodeficiency Virus, Hepatitis B Virus and Hepatitis C Virus Control and Elimination Policies, Regulations, and Strategies in Ethiopia.
作者： Mequanente Dagnaw, Bisrat Misganaw Geremew, Lemma Derseh Gezie
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 人类免疫缺陷病毒 (HIV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 仍然是全球和埃塞俄比亚的主要公共卫生挑战。尽管已经制定了政策、法规和战略来支持控制和消除这些问题,但将这些政策承诺转化为有效实施仍然具有挑战性。了解利益相关者的经验和观点对于确定政策意识、参与、实施和项目协调方面的差距非常重要。目的 本研究旨在探讨影响埃塞俄比亚艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎控制和消除政策、法规和战略的制定、实施和有效性的挑战和机遇。方法 在参与艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎政策制定、实施、监管、监测、研究和服务提供的利益相关者中进行了基于设施和机构的定性研究。三十四名参与者是根据他们的专业角色、经验、知识和参与相关项目的情况而有目的地选择的。数据收集时间为 2023 年 4 月 17 日至 5 月 27 日,采用定性访谈和焦点小组讨论,并辅之以对相关政策、战略、指南和计划文件的审查以及对选定服务提供和制药环境的观察。使用 MAXQDA Analytics Pro 2024 对数据进行转录、必要时翻译和主题分析。分析重点是政策意识、利益相关者参与、利益相关者角色、实施状态、机遇和挑战。结果 参与者的平均工作经验为 16.82 年(SD = 0.79),其中 30 名(88.2%)为男性。在 34 名参与者中,12 名(35.3%)明确指出或描述了 HIV、HBV 和 HCV 控制和消除政策、法规和策略的存在及其主要特征,而 22 名(64.7%)则没有。15 名 (44.1%) 参与者表示利益相关者参与了政策和战略制定,而 19 名 (55.9%) 则表示没有足够的参与机会。大多数参与者(32 名(94.1%))表现出对利益相关者在政策制定和实施中的作用和责任的理解。然而,只有 8 名(23.5%)参与者报告了明确的当前实施状态或进展情况,而 26 名(76.5%)参与者报告没有明确的实施状态。二十九名 (85.3%) 参与者报告说,加强 HIV、HBV 和 HCV 控制和消除的机会有限或不明确。几乎所有参与者(32 名(94.1%))都指出了影响政策和战略实施的挑战,包括与 COVID-19 相关的干扰以及对政策和战略转化为实践的担忧。结论 研究结果表明,埃塞俄比亚对利益相关者责任的认识与艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎控制和消除政策、法规和战略的实际认识和实施之间存在巨大差距。加强政策传播、包容性利益相关者参与、实施监测、协调以及将政策承诺转化为服务提供活动可能有助于实现国家和全球消除目标。可持续融资、加强卫生系统能力、利益相关者问责制以及可靠的监测和评估系统是改进实施的重要考虑因素。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究旨在探讨影响埃塞俄比亚艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎控制和消除政策、法规和战略的制定、实施和有效性的挑战和机遇。重点是政策意识、利益相关者的参与、角色、实施状况以及将政策承诺转化为服务提供活动。
证据基础与方法
在参与艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和丙型肝炎政策制定、实施、监管、监测、研究和服务提供的利益相关者中进行了一项基于设施和机构的定性研究。数据收集时间为 2023 年 4 月 17 日至 5 月 27 日,采用定性访谈和焦点小组讨论,并辅以对相关政策、战略、指南和计划文件的审查以及对选定服务提供和制药环境的观察。使用 MAXQDA Analytics Pro 2024 对数据进行转录、必要时翻译和主题分析。
共识与结论
大多数参与者(94.1%)表现出对利益相关者在政策制定和实施中的作用和责任的理解。然而,只有 23.5% 的受访者明确表示了当前的实施情况或进展,这表明角色认知与实际实施之间存在差距。调查结果表明,政策意识和实施之间存在巨大差距,面临的挑战包括 COVID-19 大流行的干扰以及将政策转化为实践的困难。
争议与缺口
85.3%的参与者报告称,加强HIV、HBV和HCV的控制与消除存在有限或不明确的机会。此外,仅有35.3%的参与者清楚地识别或描述了HIV、HBV和HCV控制与消除政策、法规和战略的存在及其关键特征,凸显了许多利益相关方在意识和理解方面的缺乏。
科研与实践启示
加强政策传播、包容性利益相关方参与、实施监测、协调以及将政策承诺转化为服务交付活动,可能有助于实现国家和全球消除目标。可持续的资金、加强卫生系统能力、利益相关方问责制以及可靠的监测和评估系统是改善实施的重要考虑因素。

Challenges and Opportunities of Human Immunodeficiency Virus, Hepatitis B Virus and Hepatitis C Virus Control and Elimination Policies, Regulations, and Strategies in Ethiopia.

Authors: Mequanente Dagnaw, Bisrat Misganaw Geremew, Lemma Derseh Gezie
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Human immunodeficiency virus (HIV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), and hepatitis C virus (HCV) remain major public health challenges globally and in Ethiopia. Although policies, regulations, and strategies have been developed to support their control and elimination, translating these policy commitments into effective implementation remains challenging. Understanding stakeholder experiences and perspectives is important for identifying gaps in policy awareness, participation, implementation, and program coordination. Objective This study aimed to explore the challenges and opportunities affecting the development, implementation, and effectiveness of HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination policies, regulations, and strategies in Ethiopia. Methods A facility‐ and institution‐based qualitative study was conducted among stakeholders involved in HIV, HBV, and HCV policy development, implementation, regulation, monitoring, research, and service delivery. Thirty‐four participants were purposively selected based on their professional roles, experience, knowledge, and involvement in relevant programs. Data were collected from April 17 to May 27, 2023, using qualitative interviews and focus group discussions, complemented by a review of relevant policy, strategy, guideline, and program documents and observations of selected service‐delivery and pharmaceutical settings. Data were transcribed, translated where necessary, and thematically analysed using MAXQDA Analytics Pro 2024. The analysis focused on policy awareness, stakeholder involvement, stakeholder roles, implementation status, opportunities, and challenges. Results Participants had a mean work experience of 16.82 years (SD = 0.79), and 30 (88.2%) were male. Of the 34 participants, 12 (35.3%) clearly identified or described the existence and key features of HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination policies, regulations, and strategies, while 22 (64.7%) did not. Fifteen (44.1%) participants reported stakeholder involvement in policy and strategy development, whereas 19 (55.9%) did not report adequate opportunities for participation. Most participants, 32 (94.1%), demonstrated an understanding of stakeholder roles and responsibilities in policy development and implementation. However, only 8 (23.5%) participants reported a clear current implementation status or progress, while 26 (76.5%) reported no clear implementation status. Twenty‐nine (85.3%) participants reported limited or unclear opportunities for strengthening HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination. Nearly all participants, 32 (94.1%), identified challenges affecting policy and strategy implementation, including COVID‐19‐related disruptions and concerns regarding the translation of policies and strategies into practice. Conclusion The findings indicate a substantial gap between recognition of stakeholder responsibilities and the practical awareness and implementation of HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination policies, regulations, and strategies in Ethiopia. Strengthening policy dissemination, inclusive stakeholder engagement, implementation monitoring, coordination, and the translation of policy commitments into service‐delivery activities may support progress toward national and global elimination targets. Sustainable financing, strengthened health‐system capacity, stakeholder accountability, and reliable monitoring and evaluation systems are important considerations for improving implementation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study aimed to explore the challenges and opportunities affecting the development, implementation, and effectiveness of HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination policies, regulations, and strategies in Ethiopia. The focus was on policy awareness, stakeholder involvement, roles, implementation status, and the translation of policy commitments into service-delivery activities.
Evidence base and method
A facility- and institution-based qualitative study was conducted among stakeholders involved in HIV, HBV, and HCV policy development, implementation, regulation, monitoring, research, and service delivery. Data were collected from April 17 to May 27, 2023, using qualitative interviews and focus group discussions, complemented by a review of relevant policy, strategy, guideline, and program documents and observations of selected service-delivery and pharmaceutical settings. Data were transcribed, translated where necessary, and thematically analysed using MAXQDA Analytics Pro 2024.
Consensus and conclusions
Most participants (94.1%) demonstrated an understanding of stakeholder roles and responsibilities in policy development and implementation. However, only 23.5% reported a clear current implementation status or progress, indicating a gap between recognition of roles and practical implementation. The findings suggest a substantial gap between policy awareness and implementation, with challenges including disruptions from the COVID-19 pandemic and difficulties in translating policies into practice.
Controversies and gaps
There was limited or unclear opportunities for strengthening HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination, as reported by 85.3% of participants. Additionally, only 35.3% of participants clearly identified or described the existence and key features of HIV, HBV, and HCV control and elimination policies, regulations, and strategies, highlighting a lack of awareness and understanding among many stakeholders.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening policy dissemination, inclusive stakeholder engagement, implementation monitoring, coordination, and the translation of policy commitments into service-delivery activities may support progress toward national and global elimination targets. Sustainable financing, strengthened health-system capacity, stakeholder accountability, and reliable monitoring and evaluation systems are important considerations for improving implementation.

新型治疗在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)中实现功能性治愈(功能性治愈)的临床前景

Clinical Prospect of Novel Therapies for Functional Cure in Chronic Hepatitis B.
作者： Karen Cheuk‐Ying Ho, Rex Wan‐Hin Hui, Lung‐Yi Mak, Man‐Fung Yuen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题。功能性治愈(功能性治愈)被定义为在停药后24周持续实现乙肝表面抗原(HBsAg)血清学清除且乙肝病毒(HBV)DNA不可检测。它已成为一个具有临床意义的终点,与肝纤维化消退、肝细胞癌(HCC)风险降低以及较低的治疗负担相关。然而,尽管当前CHB治疗手段(包括核苷(酸)类似物(NUCs)和聚乙二醇干扰素-α(Peg-IFNα))能够有效抑制病毒,但很少能实现功能性治愈,导致相当一部分患者仍面临不良肝相关结局的风险,包括肝功能失代偿和HCC。因此,新型治疗药物旨在加速实现功能性治愈。这些药物被分为病毒靶向治疗和免疫调节治疗。方法 本综述总结了针对实现功能性治愈的新兴疗法的临床证据,特别关注RNA靶向疗法。同时,还讨论了与治疗选择相关的实际考虑因素。结果 在这些新型疗法中,RNA靶向疗法目前最具前景,其中贝皮罗韦森(一种非连接的反义寡核苷酸)是唯一完成III期临床试验并显示出有效结果的药物。越来越多的研究正在探索以RNA靶向疗法为基础的联合方案,利用潜在的协同效应以提高HBsAg血清学清除率。结论 RNA靶向疗法和联合方案为实现慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)的功能性治愈(功能性治愈)提供了有希望的途径。未来的研究应着重于这些新型药物的长期疗效和安全性。

Clinical Prospect of Novel Therapies for Functional Cure in Chronic Hepatitis B.

Authors: Karen Cheuk‐Ying Ho, Rex Wan‐Hin Hui, Lung‐Yi Mak, Man‐Fung Yuen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a major global health problem. Functional cure is defined as sustained hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance with undetectable HBV DNA 24 weeks after cessation of therapy. It has emerged as a clinically meaningful endpoint, associated with regression of liver fibrosis, reduced hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk, and a lower treatment burden. However, current CHB therapies, including nucleos(t)ide analogues (NUCs) and pegylated interferon‐α (Peg‐IFNα), rarely lead to functional cure despite adequate viral suppression, leading to a notable portion of patients remaining at risk for adverse liver‐related outcomes, including hepatic decompensation and HCC. Hence, novel therapeutic agents are aimed at accelerating towards functional cure. They are categorized as virus‐targeting and immunomodulatory therapies. Methods This review summarizes the clinical evidence for emerging therapies aimed at acheiving functional cure, with a particular focus on RNA‐targeting therapies. It also discusses practical considerations relevant to treatment selection. Results Among these novel therapies, RNA‐targeting therapies are currently the most promising, with bepirovirsen (an unconjugated antisense oligonucleotide) standing out as the only agent completed in Phase III study showing efficacious results. Combination regimens that use RNA‐targeting therapies as backbone are increasingly pursued, leveraging potential synergistic effects to achieve higher rates of HBsAg seroclearance. Conclusion RNA‐targetting therapies and combination regimens offer promising approaches in attaining functional cure in CHB. Further research should emphasize on the long‐term efficacy and safety for these novel agents.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a major global health problem. Functional cure is defined as sustained hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance with undetectable HBV DNA 24 weeks after cessation of therapy. It has emerged as a clinically meaningful endpoint, associated with regression of liver fibrosis, reduced hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) risk, and a lower treatment burden.
Evidence base and method
This review summarizes the clinical evidence for emerging therapies aimed at achieving functional cure, with a particular focus on RNA-targeting therapies. They are categorized as virus-targeting and immunomodulatory therapies.
Consensus and conclusions
Among these novel therapies, RNA-targeting therapies are currently the most promising, with bepirovirsen (an unconjugated antisense oligonucleotide) standing out as the only agent completed in Phase III study showing efficacious results. Combination regimens that use RNA-targeting therapies as backbone are increasingly pursued, leveraging potential synergistic effects to achieve higher rates of HBsAg seroclearance. RNA-targeting therapies and combination regimens offer promising approaches in attaining functional cure in CHB.
Controversies and gaps
Current CHB therapies, including nucleos(t)ide analogues (NUCs) and pegylated interferon-α (Peg-IFNα), rarely lead to functional cure despite adequate viral suppression, leading to a notable portion of patients remaining at risk for adverse liver-related outcomes, including hepatic decompensation and HCC. Further research should emphasize on the long-term efficacy and safety for these novel agents.
Research and practice implications
It also discusses practical considerations relevant to treatment selection.

HIV与病毒性肝炎共感染的现状与未来

The present and future of HIV and viral hepatitis co-infection.
作者： Oana Săndulescu, Christoph Boesecke, Sanjay Bhagani, Mojca Matičič, Chien-Ching Hung, Jürgen K Rockstroh, Juan Ambrosioni
摘要中文翻译HIV与肝炎病毒共感染仍然是全球主要的公共卫生挑战,其原因包括共同的传播途径、具有重叠获得方式的人群以及可能加剧的疾病进展。HIV与乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)的共感染仍然普遍,尤其是在流行区生活的人群、关键人群、注射毒品者、男男性行为者以及来自流行区的移民中。抗逆转录病毒治疗正朝着不含HBV活性的方案转变,这使得某些个体感染或重新激活HBV的风险增加。尽管直接作用抗病毒药物(DAAs)在许多高收入地区显著降低了HCV的活动性感染,但残留的疾病负担、再感染以及对治疗的不平等获取仍然存在。肝炎delta病毒(HDV)继续作为HIV-HBV共感染个体中一种严重但未被充分诊断的共感染存在,且全球关于其诊断的数据仍不充分。急性甲型肝炎和戊型肝炎越来越多地影响HIV感染者,表现为与HIV相关的病例和散发病例,特别是在血清学监测覆盖率不足的背景下。当代管理强调系统性筛查、血清学监测、整合治疗策略以及公共卫生干预措施,以减少HIV感染者与肝炎相关的发病率和死亡率。

共识与结论
抗逆转录病毒治疗正朝着不含HBV活性的方案转变,这使得某些个体感染或重新激活HBV的风险增加。

The present and future of HIV and viral hepatitis co-infection.

Authors: Oana Săndulescu, Christoph Boesecke, Sanjay Bhagani, Mojca Matičič, Chien-Ching Hung, Jürgen K Rockstroh, Juan Ambrosioni
Original abstract (English)
HIV and hepatitis virus co-infection remains a major global health challenge, driven by shared transmission routes, populations with overlapping modes of acquisition, and potentially compounded disease progression. Co-infections of HIV and hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) remain prevalent, particularly among people who live in endemic areas, key populations, people who inject drugs, men who have sex with men, and migrants from endemic regions. Antiretroviral therapy is shifting towards regimens without HBV activity, increasing the risk for HBV infection or reactivation in some individuals. Although direct-acting antivirals have reduced active HCV infection considerably in many high-income settings, residual disease burden, reinfections, and inequitable access to treatment persist. Hepatitis delta virus continues to represent a severe but underdiagnosed co-infection among individuals with HIV-HBV co-infection, with insufficient global surveillance data. Acute hepatitis A and E increasingly affect people with HIV, presenting both as outbreak-related and sporadic cases, particularly in the context of suboptimal vaccination coverage. Contemporary management emphasises systematic screening, vaccination, integrated antiviral strategies, and public health interventions to reduce liver-related morbidity and mortality in people with HIV.
View review five-element analysis

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
Although direct-acting antivirals have reduced active HCV infection considerably in many high-income settings, residual disease burden, reinfections, and inequitable access to treatment persist.
Research and practice implications

中国乙型肝炎病毒相关肝细胞癌和EBV相关鼻咽癌的免疫药理学进展:机制、疗法与临床视角

Immunopharmacological Advances in HBV Associated Hepatocellular Carcinoma and EBV-Related Nasopharyngeal Carcinoma in China: Mechanisms, Therapies, and Clinical Perspectives.
作者： Xiaochen Liu, Ligen Wang
摘要中文翻译致癌病毒仍然是中国癌症的重要病因,其中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和埃普斯坦-巴尔病毒(EBV)导致的恶性肿瘤占很大比例。HBV是中国肝细胞癌(HCC)的主要病因,约占病例的85-92%。EBV与非角化未分化型鼻咽癌(NPC)密切相关,尤其是在流行地区。传统的抗病毒治疗手段,如手术、放疗和化疗,在疾病晚期效果有限,凸显了需要引入更有效的抗病毒治疗策略的必要性。免疫药理学干预已成为一种革命性的治疗方式。本文综述了HBV相关HCC和EBV相关NPC的现有免疫药理学研究,包括肿瘤免疫微环境的调控机制、基于免疫的治疗机制以及中国在临床方面的重大进展。国内自主研发的抗PD-1抗体,如信迪利单抗(sintilimab)、康宁哲(camrelizumab)、替雷利珠单抗(tislelizumab)和拓扑替康(toripalimab),已通过大规模临床试验获得监管批准,并目前作为不可切除HCC和复发或转移性NPC的一线治疗手段。同时,使用EBV抗原(包括EBV特异性细胞毒性T淋巴细胞和工程化T细胞受体策略)的过继细胞疗法显示出良好的临床潜力。此外,免疫检查点阻断与靶向治疗或局部区域治疗的联合方案在HBV感染者中显示出更好的生存率。尽管取得了这些进展,仍存在一些问题,如治疗耐药性、免疫相关不良事件以及生物标志物表现不一致。未来的研究应重点放在基于生物标志物的治疗决策、联合方案优化以及新型细胞疗法的更广泛应用。

Immunopharmacological Advances in HBV Associated Hepatocellular Carcinoma and EBV-Related Nasopharyngeal Carcinoma in China: Mechanisms, Therapies, and Clinical Perspectives.

Authors: Xiaochen Liu, Ligen Wang
Original abstract (English)
Oncogenic viruses continue to be a significant cause of cancer in China, with a significant proportion of malignancies caused by hepatitis B virus (HBV) and Epstein-Barr virus (EBV). HBV is the dominant etiological factor for HCC in China, accounting for approximately 85-92% of cases. EBV is closely implicated in non-keratinizing undifferentiated nasopharyngeal carcinoma (NPC), especially in endemic areas. The use of conventional treatment methods, such as surgery, radiotherapy, and chemotherapy, has not been very effective, especially at advanced stages of the disease, underscoring the need to incorporate more effective treatment approaches. Immunopharmacological interventions have emerged as a revolutionary practice in this regard. This review provides an overview of existing immunopharmacology in HBV-related HCC and EBV-associated NPC, including how the tumour immune microenvironment is modulated, the mechanisms of action of immune-based therapies, and the major clinical advances in China. Domestically developed anti-PD-1 antibodies, such as sintilimab, camrelizumab, tislelizumab, and toripalimab, have received regulatory approval based on large-scale clinical trials and are currently used as first-line treatment for unresectable HCC and for recurrent or metastatic NPC. Simultaneously, adoptive cellular therapies using EBV antigens (including EBV-specific cytotoxic T lymphocytes and engineered T-cell receptor strategies) have shown promising clinical potential. Moreover, combination regimens with immune checkpoint blockade and targeted therapies or locoregional therapies have demonstrated better survival rates, especially in HBV-infected people. Despite these improvements, there are still issues, such as therapeutic resistance, immune-related adverse events, and inconsistent biomarker performance. Future initiatives must focus on biomarker-directed treatment decisions, combination regimen optimization, and the wider clinical use of new cellular therapies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Simultaneously, adoptive cellular therapies using EBV antigens (including EBV-specific cytotoxic T lymphocytes and engineered T-cell receptor strategies) have shown promising clinical potential.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
This review provides an overview of existing immunopharmacology in HBV-related HCC and EBV-associated NPC, including how the tumour immune microenvironment is modulated, the mechanisms of action of immune-based therapies, and the major clinical advances in China.
Controversies and gaps
Despite these improvements, there are still issues, such as therapeutic resistance, immune-related adverse events, and inconsistent biomarker performance.
Research and practice implications
Future initiatives must focus on biomarker-directed treatment decisions, combination regimen optimization, and the wider clinical use of new cellular therapies.

肝炎D的新范式:筛查、治疗以及治愈之路。

New paradigms in Hepatitis D: screening, treatment, and the path to cure.
作者： Toni Herta, Christian Gaebler, Johannes Wiegand, Münevver Demir, Frank Tacke
摘要中文翻译慢性肝炎D病毒感染,也称为慢性肝炎delta,仍然是慢性病毒性肝炎中最严重的形式,与肝病进展加速、肝硬化、失代偿和肝细胞癌有关。近年来,肝炎D的筛查策略和抗病毒药物治疗取得了重大进展。本文综述了支持在乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性人群中进行更广泛肝炎D筛查的证据,并讨论了在不同流行病学环境下实施筛查的实用考虑因素,同时回顾了当前和新兴的治疗治疗方法——包括进入抑制剂(bulevirtide)、前酰化抑制剂(lonafarnib)、免疫调节治疗(聚乙二醇干扰素)以及针对乙型肝炎表面抗原的方法(tobevibart、elebsiran、brelovitug、核酸聚合物),重点探讨治疗反应及病毒抑制的持续性。总之,新兴疗法显著提高了抗病毒药物的疗效,使肝炎D的管理重点从针对治疗的抑制转向持久的非治疗控制和功能性治愈,从而改变了诊断、治疗以及慢性肝炎D的长期管理。
查看综述五要素

本文综述了支持在乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性人群中进行更广泛肝炎D筛查的证据,并讨论了在不同流行病学环境下实施筛查的实用考虑因素,同时回顾了当前和新兴的治疗治疗方法——包括进入抑制剂(bulevirtide)、前酰化抑制剂(lonafarnib)、免疫调节治疗(聚乙二醇干扰素)以及针对乙型肝炎表面抗原的方法(tobevibart、elebsiran、brelovitug、核酸聚合物),重点探讨治疗反应及病毒抑制的持续性。

总之,新兴疗法显著提高了抗病毒药物的疗效,使肝炎D的管理重点从针对治疗的抑制转向持久的非治疗控制和功能性治愈,从而改变了诊断、治疗以及慢性肝炎D的长期管理。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
提供的证据没有明确说明具体的研究或实践意义。

New paradigms in Hepatitis D: screening, treatment, and the path to cure.

Authors: Toni Herta, Christian Gaebler, Johannes Wiegand, Münevver Demir, Frank Tacke
Original abstract (English)
Chronic hepatitis D virus infection, also known as chronic hepatitis delta, remains the most severe form of chronic viral hepatitis, associated with accelerated liver disease progression, cirrhosis, decompensation, and hepatocellular carcinoma. Recent years have seen major advances in screening strategies and antiviral therapies for hepatitis D. This review discusses evidence supporting broader hepatitis D screening among hepatitis B surface antigen-positive individuals and practical considerations for implementation across epidemiological settings, and reviews current and emerging treatments - entry inhibition (bulevirtide), prenylation inhibition (lonafarnib), immune-modulatory therapies (pegylated interferons), and hepatitis B surface antigen-targeting approaches (tobevibart, elebsiran, brelovitug, nucleic acid polymers) - focusing on treatment response and durability of viral suppression. In conclusion, emerging therapies have substantially improved antiviral efficacy, shifting the focus of hepatitis D management from on-treatment suppression toward durable off-treatment control and functional cure, changing the diagnosis, treatment, and long-term management of chronic hepatitis D.

雄激素受体信号在肝细胞癌中的作用:男性优势机制与治疗机会

Androgen receptor signaling in hepatocellular carcinoma: mechanisms of male predominance and therapeutic opportunities.
作者： Yang Zhou, Yi-Yu Qin
摘要中文翻译肝细胞癌(HCC)在男性中的发病率是女性的两到四倍。病毒性肝炎、酒精使用和肥胖的性别分布仅能解释部分这种差异,这表明性别特异性宿主生物学可能是进一步的驱动因素。雄激素受体(AR),一种在肝细胞和大多数HCC中表达的配体激活转录因子,是一个强有力的候选者:流行病学和实验数据均表明其与男性发病率较高有关。本综述汇总了这些证据。前瞻性队列研究研究将较高的循环睾酮和性激素结合球蛋白与肝癌风险联系起来,尽管实验性升高SHBG会抑制而非促进肝癌发生,这种不一致影响着如何解读这些关联。实验研究将潜在的生物学机制分为几个模块:持续激活的AR及其剪接变体、受激酶驱动的受体磷酸化、增殖、侵袭和血管生成的下游效应器、非编码RNA回路、雄激素代谢酶、系统内分泌轴以及雄激素对肿瘤免疫微环境的影响。每个模块的权重在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关、丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)相关、代谢功能障碍相关和类固醇诱导的疾病中有所不同。治疗研究仍主要处于临床前阶段,集中在AR拮抗剂与酪氨酸激酶抑制剂或免疫检查点阻断的联合使用、针对AR剪接变体的降解剂、靶向蛋白降解的嵌合体以及消除配体独立受体状态的核酸方法,以及酶抑制剂上。两个问题仍然限制了转化:AR在大多数情况下具有致癌性,但在某些情况下具有肿瘤抑制作用;雄激素在某些情况下会抑制抗肿瘤免疫,而在其他情况下则增强它。明确每种情况何时发生,将决定AR靶向治疗是否能从前列腺癌扩展到肝癌。

Androgen receptor signaling in hepatocellular carcinoma: mechanisms of male predominance and therapeutic opportunities.

Authors: Yang Zhou, Yi-Yu Qin
Original abstract (English)
Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is two to four times more common in men than in women. The sex distribution of viral hepatitis, alcohol use and adiposity explains only part of this difference, which points to sex-specific host biology as a further driver. The androgen receptor (AR), a ligand-activated transcription factor expressed in hepatocytes and in most HCC, is a strong candidate: both epidemiologic and experimental data implicate it in the male excess. This review draws that evidence together. Prospective cohorts link higher circulating testosterone and sex hormone-binding globulin to liver cancer risk, although raising SHBG experimentally suppresses rather than promotes hepatocarcinogenesis, a discordance that bears on how those associations should be read, and experimental studies group the underlying biology into several modules: constitutively active AR and its splice variants, kinase-driven phosphorylation of the receptor, downstream effectors of proliferation, invasion and angiogenesis, non-coding RNA circuits, androgen-metabolizing enzymes and the systemic endocrine axis, and the effect of androgen on the tumor immune microenvironment. The weight of each module differs across hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related, hepatitis C virus (HCV)-related, metabolic dysfunction-associated and anabolic steroid-induced disease. Therapeutic work remains largely preclinical and has centered on AR antagonists combined with tyrosine kinase inhibitors or immune checkpoint blockade, on degraders aimed at AR splice variants, on proteolysis-targeting chimeras and nucleic-acid approaches that eliminate ligand-independent receptor states, and on enzyme inhibitors. Two problems still limit translation: AR appears oncogenic in most settings but tumor-suppressive in some, and androgen suppresses antitumor immunity in certain contexts while enhancing it in others. Clarifying when each holds is what will determine whether AR-directed therapy, already established in prostate cancer, can be extended to liver cancer.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is two to four times more common in men than in women. The sex distribution of viral hepatitis, alcohol use and adiposity explains only part of this difference, which points to sex-specific host biology as a further driver. The androgen receptor (AR), a ligand-activated transcription factor expressed in hepatocytes and in most HCC, is a strong candidate: both epidemiologic and experimental data implicate it in the male excess.

Consensus and conclusions
The androgen receptor (AR) is implicated in the male excess of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Experimental studies group the underlying biology into several modules, including constitutively active AR and its splice variants, kinase-driven phosphorylation of the receptor, downstream effectors of proliferation, invasion and angiogenesis, non-coding RNA circuits, androgen-metabolizing enzymes and the systemic endocrine axis, and the effect of androgen on the tumor immune microenvironment.
Controversies and gaps
The weight of each module differs across hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related, hepatitis C virus (HCV)-related, metabolic dysfunction-associated and anabolic steroid-induced disease. Two problems still limit translation: AR appears oncogenic in most settings but tumor-suppressive in some, and androgen suppresses antitumor immunity in certain contexts while enhancing it in others.
Research and practice implications
Clarifying when each holds is what will determine whether AR-directed therapy, already established in prostate cancer, can be extended to liver cancer. Therapeutic work remains largely preclinical and has centered on AR antagonists combined with tyrosine kinase inhibitors or immune checkpoint blockade, on degraders aimed at AR splice variants, on proteolysis-targeting chimeras and nucleic-acid approaches that eliminate ligand-independent receptor states, and on enzyme inhibitors.

乙型肝炎病毒 在 FOLFIRINOX 化疗和粒细胞集落刺激因子治疗晚期胰腺癌中的再激活 病例报告

Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation During FOLFIRINOX Chemotherapy and Granulocyte Colony-Stimulating Factor in Advanced Pancreatic Cancer Case Report
作者： Marina Girgis, Majid Michael, Zahin Alam, Riddhi R Machchhar, Christopher Meusburger, Jay A Vida, Marina Girgis BS, Majid Michael BS, Jay A Vida DO
摘要中文翻译摘要 乙型肝炎病毒再激活是免疫抑制治疗中一个众所周知的并发症,最常报告于接受强效方案治疗的血液系统恶性肿瘤患者中。相比之下,实体瘤患者中报告的乙型肝炎病毒再激活较少,尤其是胰腺癌患者。关于多药联合治疗(如 FOLFIRINOX)对乙型肝炎病毒再激活的潜在影响的数据有限。粒细胞集落刺激因子(G-CSF)常作为辅助治疗使用,但其在乙型肝炎病毒再激活中的作用尚不清楚。我们报告了一例晚期胰腺癌合并肝转移患者在接受改良 FOLFIRINOX 和聚乙二醇化重组人粒细胞集落刺激因子治疗后发生乙型肝炎病毒再激活的病例。该患者于 2025 年 6 月 2 日出现疲劳、虚弱、周围水肿和白细胞增多,这是在 5 月 21 日接受改良 FOLFIRINOX 治疗和 5 月 23 日接受聚乙二醇化重组人粒细胞集落刺激因子治疗后的第 2 个周期。由于患者耐受性差,住院期间暂停了 FOLFIRINOX 治疗。住院期间肝酶升高,乙型肝炎检测显示反应性 HBsAg 和总抗-HBc,非反应性抗-HBc IgM,抗-HBs <5 mIU/mL,以及 HBV DNA 3,760,000 IU/mL(log 6.58)。回顾既往记录显示,在化疗前 HBsAg 阳性,但抗-HBc IgM 阴性,且未获得基线 HBV DNA 检测结果,也未记录 抗病毒药物 预防措施。该患者于 6 月 7 日出院,并于 6 月 18 日由门诊肿瘤科开始每日服用恩替卡韦 0.5 mg。目前指南建议在系统性抗癌治疗前进行 HBV 筛查,并对 HBsAg 阳性患者进行 抗病毒药物 预防。因此,该病例强调了在接受化疗的实体瘤患者中实施现有推荐进行完整 HBV 筛查、基线 HBV DNA 评估和 抗病毒药物 预防措施的重要性。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
乙型肝炎病毒 再激活是免疫抑制治疗中一个众所周知的并发症,最常报告于接受强效方案治疗的血液系统恶性肿瘤患者中。本 病例报告 重点探讨了接受改良 FOLFIRINOX 和聚乙二醇化重组人粒细胞集落刺激因子治疗的晚期胰腺癌患者中 HBV 再激活的情况。
证据基础与方法
本回顾基于单一 病例报告。研究设计未明确描述,但报告包含详细的临床叙述和实验室检查结果。证据未进行汇总或荟萃分析,也未报告特定的回顾方法。
共识与结论
本 病例报告 表明 乙型肝炎病毒 再激活可能在接受 FOLFIRINOX 和粒细胞集落刺激因子化疗的晚期胰腺癌患者中发生。它强调了在这些患者中进行 HBV 筛查和 抗病毒药物 预防措施的重要性。
争议与缺口
证据仅限于单一 病例报告,无法得出普遍适用的结论。目前尚无关于接受 FOLFIRINOX 或粒细胞集落刺激因子治疗的胰腺癌患者中 HBV 再激活频率的数据。此外,未进行基线 HBV DNA 检测,也未记录 抗病毒药物 预防措施,这可能影响病例的解读。
科研与实践启示
该病例强调了遵循当前指南,即在系统性抗癌治疗前进行 HBV 筛查,并对 HBsAg 阳性患者进行 抗病毒药物 预防措施的重要性。它还表明需要进一步研究,以评估接受 FOLFIRINOX 和粒细胞集落刺激因子治疗的胰腺癌患者中 HBV 再激活的风险,包括基线 HBV DNA 检测和 抗病毒药物 预防措施方案。

Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation During FOLFIRINOX Chemotherapy and Granulocyte Colony-Stimulating Factor in Advanced Pancreatic Cancer Case Report

Authors: Marina Girgis, Majid Michael, Zahin Alam, Riddhi R Machchhar, Christopher Meusburger, Jay A Vida, Marina Girgis BS, Majid Michael BS, Jay A Vida DO
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Hepatitis B reactivation is a well-recognized complication of immunosuppressive therapy, most commonly reported in patients with hematologic malignancies receiving potent regimens. In contrast, reports of reactivation occur less commonly in solid malignancies, especially pancreatic cancer. Data are limited regarding the potential impact of multidrug therapies, such as FOLFIRINOX. Granulocyte colony-stimulating factor (G-CSF) is frequently used adjunctively, but its role in HBV reactivation is unknown. We report a case of hepatitis B reactivation in a patient with advanced pancreatic cancer with metastasis to the liver receiving modified FOLFIRINOX and pegfilgrastim. The patient presented on June 2, 2025, with fatigue, weakness, peripheral edema, and leukocytosis after cycle 2 of modified FOLFIRINOX on May 21 and pegfilgrastim on May 23. FOLFIRINOX was held during admission because of poor tolerance. Liver enzymes rose during hospitalization, and hepatitis testing demonstrated reactive HBsAg and total anti-HBc, nonreactive anti-HBc IgM, anti-HBs <5 mIU/mL, and HBV DNA 3,760,000 IU/mL (log 6.58). Review of prior records demonstrated HBsAg positivity and negative anti-HBc IgM before chemotherapy, but baseline HBV DNA was not obtained and antiviral prophylaxis was not documented. He was discharged on June 7 and was subsequently started on entecavir 0.5 mg daily by outpatient oncology on June 18. Current guidelines recommend HBV screening before systemic anticancer therapy and antiviral prophylaxis for HBsAg-positive patients receiving systemic therapy. Thus, this case highlights the importance of implementing existing recommendations for complete HBV screening, baseline HBV DNA assessment, and antiviral prophylaxis in patients with solid tumors receiving chemotherapy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Hepatitis B virus reactivation is a well-recognized complication of immunosuppressive therapy, most commonly reported in patients with hematologic malignancies receiving potent regimens. This case report focuses on HBV reactivation in a patient with advanced pancreatic cancer receiving modified FOLFIRINOX and pegfilgrastim.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a single case report. The study design is not explicitly described, but the report includes a detailed clinical narrative and laboratory findings. The evidence is not pooled or meta-analyzed, and no specific review methodology is reported.
Consensus and conclusions
The case report demonstrates that hepatitis B virus reactivation can occur in patients with advanced pancreatic cancer undergoing chemotherapy with FOLFIRINOX and G-CSF. It highlights the importance of HBV screening and antiviral prophylaxis in such patients.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited to a single case report, which does not provide generalizable conclusions. There is no data on the frequency of HBV reactivation in pancreatic cancer patients receiving FOLFIRINOX or G-CSF. Additionally, baseline HBV DNA testing was not performed, and antiviral prophylaxis was not documented, which may affect the interpretation of the case.
Research and practice implications
This case underscores the need for adherence to current guidelines recommending HBV screening before systemic anticancer therapy and antiviral prophylaxis for HBsAg-positive patients. It suggests that further research is required to assess the risk of HBV reactivation in pancreatic cancer patients receiving FOLFIRINOX and G-CSF, including baseline HBV DNA testing and antiviral prophylaxis protocols.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

peginterferon alfa-2b治疗慢性乙型肝炎期间的血细胞纵向下降:临床安全性及探索性全血转录组学分析

Longitudinal hematologic decline during peginterferon alfa-2b therapy in chronic hepatitis B: clinical safety and exploratory whole-blood transcriptomics
作者： Jie Li, Wei-Lai Chen, Jing-Gu Chen, Jia-Yi Xing, Qi Chen, Jian-Hua Zhang, Rui-Xin Shi, Chen-Wei Pan, Yong-Ping Chen
摘要中文翻译基于peginterferon的慢性乙型肝炎治疗已知会导致白细胞减少、中性粒细胞减少和血小板减少,但早期血细胞轨迹与血清学反应、支持性管理及全血分子信号之间的关系仍不确定。我们对一项前瞻性、单中心的21例接受peginterferon alfa-2b治疗的队列研究进行了探索性血细胞安全性为中心的二次分析(13例应答者和8例非应答者)。在大致对应基线、第5周和第10周的访视中评估了白细胞计数(WBC)、中性粒细胞绝对值(ANC)和血小板计数。非参数比较补充了效应量和95%置信区间,并在锁定敏感性分析中探讨了支持性-治疗的时间。所有配对的第1次访视至第3次访视的变化在两个应答组中均为负值,而应答者与非应答者之间的估计值不够精确,未能建立特定应答组的毒性模式。第2次访视时WBC <4.0 × 10 9 /L发生在19/21例患者(90.5%;精确95%置信区间:69.6%-98.8%),被用作一种实用的临床管理阈值,而非区分性的转录组学终点。一个在口服支持日期后记录的指标并未显著改变拟合的WBC或ANC模式,但这种非随机化的敏感性分析无法估计治疗的疗效。在与手稿一致的DESeq2方差稳定变换工作流程中,未调整的主要Hallmark或候选基因家族均未达到预设的探索性BH-FDR q < 0.10阈值。由审稿人指导的xCell2敏感性分析显示,预设的中性粒细胞富集评分与测量的ANC之间存在纵向内部一致性;成分调整改变了若干通路估计值,但没有任何配对的W0-W10家族达到q < 0.10,且不稳定的基础调整血小板信号未支持特定的通路或机制。这些小型队列研究的发现是探索性的,不能作为等效性或机制的证据,需要在更大的独立队列研究中进行确认。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
基于peginterferon的慢性乙型肝炎治疗已知会导致白细胞减少、中性粒细胞减少和血小板减少,但早期血细胞轨迹与血清学反应、支持性管理及全血分子信号之间的关系仍不确定。
证据基础与方法
我们对一项前瞻性、单中心的21例接受peginterferon alfa-2b治疗的队列研究进行了探索性血细胞安全性为中心的二次分析(13例应答者和8例非应答者)。在大致对应基线、第5周和第10周的访视中评估了白细胞计数(WBC)、中性粒细胞绝对值(ANC)和血小板计数。非参数比较补充了效应量和95%置信区间,并在锁定敏感性分析中探讨了支持性-治疗的时间。
共识与结论
所有配对的第1次访视至第3次访视的变化在两个应答组中均为负值,而应答者与非应答者之间的估计值不够精确,未能建立特定应答组的毒性模式。在与手稿一致的DESeq2方差稳定变换工作流程中,未调整的主要Hallmark或候选基因家族均未达到预设的探索性BH-FDR q < 0.10阈值。
争议与缺口
一个在口服支持日期后记录的指标并未显著改变拟合的WBC或ANC模式,但这种非随机化的敏感性分析无法估计治疗的疗效。这些小型队列研究的发现是探索性的,不能作为等效性或机制的证据,需要在更大的独立队列研究中进行确认。
科研与实践启示
这些发现表明需要在更大的独立队列研究中进行进一步研究,以确认探索性结果,并更好地理解慢性乙型肝炎患者中血细胞变化与治疗结果之间的关系。

Longitudinal hematologic decline during peginterferon alfa-2b therapy in chronic hepatitis B: clinical safety and exploratory whole-blood transcriptomics

Authors: Jie Li, Wei-Lai Chen, Jing-Gu Chen, Jia-Yi Xing, Qi Chen, Jian-Hua Zhang, Rui-Xin Shi, Chen-Wei Pan, Yong-Ping Chen
Original abstract (English)
Peginterferon-based therapy for chronic hepatitis B is known to cause leukopenia, neutropenia, and thrombocytopenia, but the relationship of early hematologic trajectories to serological response, supportive management, and whole-blood molecular signals remains uncertain. We conducted an exploratory hematologic safety-focused secondary analysis of a prospective, single-center cohort of 21 patients receiving peginterferon alfa-2b-based therapy (13 responders and 8 non-responders). WBC, ANC, and platelet counts were assessed at visits corresponding approximately to baseline, Week 5, and Week 10. Non-parametric comparisons were supplemented with effect sizes and 95% confidence intervals, and supportive-treatment timing was examined in locked sensitivity analyses. All paired Visit-1-to-Visit-3 changes were negative in both response groups, whereas responder-versus-non-responder estimates were imprecise and did not establish a response-group-specific toxicity pattern. Visit-2 WBC <4.0 × 10 9 /L occurred in 19/21 patients (90.5%; exact 95% CI: 69.6%-98.8%) and was treated as a pragmatic clinical-management context threshold rather than a discriminating transcriptomic endpoint. A post-documented oral-support-date indicator did not materially alter the fitted WBC or ANC pattern, but this non-randomized sensitivity analysis cannot estimate treatment efficacy. In the manuscript-consistent DESeq2 variance-stabilizing-transformation workflow, no unadjusted primary Hallmark or candidate-gene family met the prespecified exploratory BH-FDR q < 0.10 threshold. A reviewer-directed xCell2 sensitivity analysis showed longitudinal internal concordance between the prespecified neutrophil enrichment score and measured ANC; composition adjustment changed several pathway estimates, but no paired W0-W10 family reached q < 0.10 and unstable baseline-adjusted platelet signals did not support a specific pathway or mechanism. These small-cohort findings are exploratory, are not evidence of equivalence or mechanism, and require confirmation in larger independent cohorts.

Consensus and conclusions

In the manuscript-consistent DESeq2 variance-stabilizing-transformation workflow, no unadjusted primary Hallmark or candidate-gene family met the prespecified exploratory BH-FDR q < 0.10 threshold.

These small-cohort findings are exploratory, are not evidence of equivalence or mechanism, and require confirmation in larger independent cohorts.

The findings suggest the need for further research in larger independent cohorts to confirm the exploratory results and to better understand the relationship between hematologic changes and treatment outcomes in chronic hepatitis B patients.

磁共振弹性成像和质子密度脂肪分数的优越性:代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝病和慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者肝脂肪变和纤维化的非侵入性影像学诊断的伞状荟萃分析

Superiority of magnetic resonance elastography and proton density fat fraction: an umbrella meta-analysis of non-invasive imaging for hepatic steatosis and fibrosis in metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease and chronic hepatitis B
作者： Xing-Yue Li, Da-Wei Yang, Zheng-Han Yang, Xingyue Li, Dawei Yang, Zhenghan Yang
摘要中文翻译背景:基于超声(US)和磁共振成像(MRI)的各种定量技术已被广泛用于评估肝脂肪变和纤维化。本研究旨在综合并批判性地评估系统综述中关于非侵入性影像学技术(包括瞬时弹性成像(TE)、点剪切波弹性成像(pSWE)、二维剪切波弹性成像(2D-SWE)、磁共振弹性成像(MRE)、控制衰减参数(CAP)和质子密度脂肪分数(PDFF))在代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝病(MASLD)和慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中对肝纤维化和脂肪变的诊断性能的证据。方法:从数据库建立开始,直至2025年3月2日,在PubMed、Embase、Web of Science和Cochrane Library上进行了检索,包括关键词‘肝病’、‘影像学’、‘系统综述’等。我们纳入了评估非侵入性影像学技术在MASLD或CHB患者中对肝纤维化或脂肪变诊断性能的系统综述。结果:从3752篇文章中纳入了36篇系统综述。对于肝脂肪变的评估,PDFF表现出高诊断稳定性,而CAP在轻度脂肪变的筛查中表现出良好的性能。对于肝纤维化的评估,MRE在显著(≥F2)、进展性(≥F3)纤维化和肝硬化(F4)中显示出高诊断准确性。在肥胖的MASLD患者中,MRE的特异性优于TE。在CHB患者中,MRE表现出特别高的诊断性能。其他技术(2D-SWE、TE)在特定阶段和患者群体中仍具有临床价值。结论:MRE在两种病因中的表现存在差异,表明肝硬度是一个复合生物标志物。PDFF提供了脂肪变的精确量化,而CAP适用于筛查轻度病例。研究结果强调了根据病因制定个性化影像学策略的必要性,以优化患者管理并减少对肝活检的依赖。

Superiority of magnetic resonance elastography and proton density fat fraction: an umbrella meta-analysis of non-invasive imaging for hepatic steatosis and fibrosis in metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease and chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Xing-Yue Li, Da-Wei Yang, Zheng-Han Yang, Xingyue Li, Dawei Yang, Zhenghan Yang
Original abstract (English)
Background: Various quantitative techniques based on ultrasound (US) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) have been widely used to assess hepatic steatosis and fibrosis. This study thus aimed to synthesize and critically evaluate the evidence derived from systematic reviews on the diagnostic performance of noninvasive imaging techniques including transient elastography (TE), point shear wave elastography (pSWE), two-dimensional shear wave elastography (2D-SWE), magnetic resonance elastography (MRE), controlled attenuation parameter (CAP), and proton density fat fraction (PDFF) for liver fibrosis and steatosis in patients with metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease (MASLD) and chronic hepatitis B (CHB). Methods: Searches were performed on PubMed, Embase, the Web of Science, and Cochrane Library from database inception until 2 March 2025, including keywords ‘liver diseases’, imaging’, ‘systematic review’ and others. We included systematic reviews that comprised studies evaluating the diagnostic performance of non-invasive imaging techniques for liver fibrosis or steatosis in patients with MASLD or CHB. Results: Thirty-six systematic reviews were included from 3,752 articles. For hepatic steatosis assessment, PDFF demonstrated high diagnostic stability, while CAP showed good screening performance for mild steatosis. For liver fibrosis assessment, MRE exhibited high diagnostic accuracy for significant (≥F2), advanced (≥F3) fibrosis, and cirrhosis (F4). In obese MASLD patients, MRE demonstrated superior specificity compared to TE. Among CHB patients, MRE displayed particularly high diagnostic performance. Other techniques (2D-SWE, TE) remained clinically valuable in specific stages and patient populations. Conclusions: MRE performance varied between the two etiologies, suggesting liver stiffness is a composite biomarker. PDFF provides precise quantification of steatosis, while CAP is suitable for screening mild cases. The findings emphasize the necessity of developing personalized imaging strategies based on etiology to optimize patient management and reduce reliance on liver biopsy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study thus aimed to synthesize and critically evaluate the evidence derived from systematic reviews on the diagnostic performance of noninvasive imaging techniques including transient elastography (TE), point shear wave elastography (pSWE), two-dimensional shear wave elastography (2D-SWE), magnetic resonance elastography (MRE), controlled attenuation parameter (CAP), and proton density fat fraction (PDFF) for liver fibrosis and steatosis in patients with metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease (MASLD) and chronic hepatitis B (CHB).
Evidence base and method
Searches were performed on PubMed, Embase, the Web of Science, and Cochrane Library from database inception until 2 March 2025, including keywords ‘liver diseases’, imaging’, ‘systematic review’ and others.
Consensus and conclusions
In obese MASLD patients, MRE demonstrated superior specificity compared to TE.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
PDFF provides precise quantification of steatosis, while CAP is suitable for screening mild cases.

埃塞俄比亚孕妇中流行病学感染的乙型肝炎病毒:2021年至2025年发表数据的系统综述和荟萃分析

Epidemiology of hepatitis B virus infection among pregnant women in Ethiopia: a systematic review and meta-analysis of data published between 2021 and 2025
作者： Merid Assefa Awato, Takele Tadesse, Kassa Daka, Amene Abebe, Melaku Eriso, Habtamu Elias, Eskinder Wolka
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述旨在整合现有信息,对埃塞俄比亚孕妇中乙型肝炎病毒感染情况进行准确估计,并重点关注患病率及其相关因素。
证据基础与方法
根据PRISMA 2020指南进行了系统综述和元分析。研究从多个数据库中识别,包括Google Scholar、PubMed、Embase、Web of Science、Scopus、Cochrane Library和African Journal Online,涵盖2021年1月1日至2025年12月25日的出版物。使用Joanna Briggs Institute工具评估方法学质量,并采用随机效应DerSimonian-Liard模型估计合并的患病率。使用I2评估异质性,并通过Egger’s检验和漏斗图评估发表偏倚。
共识与结论
埃塞俄比亚孕妇中乙型肝炎病毒感染的汇总患病率估计为7.3%(95% CI:6.3–8.4%),研究间存在显著异质性(I2 = 76.25%,p < 0.001)。已确定多个与HBV感染相关的因素,包括流产史、身体纹身、与HBV患者接触史、HIV阳性、牙科治疗、HBV家族史、输血史、住院史、多个性伴侣以及传统扁桃体切除术。
争议与缺口
该综述指出,目前关于埃塞俄比亚孕妇乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染的研究是零散的,且对患病率的汇总估计值尚无共识。显著的异质性(I2 = 76.25%)表明研究设计和人群存在差异,这可能影响汇总估计值的可靠性。此外,综述还指出某些方面的证据有限,例如风险因素的地理分布以及特定干预措施的影响。
科研与实践启示
建议早期检测孕妇中的HBV感染,提高对传播传播方式的认识,并实施基于证据的控制措施。需要进一步研究以解决证据缺口,特别是理解传播的机制以及干预措施在降低孕妇中HBV 患病率方面的有效性。

Epidemiology of hepatitis B virus infection among pregnant women in Ethiopia: a systematic review and meta-analysis of data published between 2021 and 2025

Authors: Merid Assefa Awato, Takele Tadesse, Kassa Daka, Amene Abebe, Melaku Eriso, Habtamu Elias, Eskinder Wolka
Original abstract (English)
Therefore, this systematic review was aimed at summarising the available information and making an accurate estimate of hepatitis B virus infection among pregnant women in Ethiopia. Methods A systematic review and meta-analysis were conducted according to the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) 2020. Results We included 25 studies conducted in Ethiopia. Conclusion A higher intermediate level of hepatitis B virus infection (2–7.99%) was observed among pregnant women in Ethiopia. Epidemiology of hepatitis B virus infection among pregnant women in Ethiopia: a systematic review and meta-analysis of data published between 2021 and 2025. History of abortion(AOR = 3.81, 95%CI: 1.69–8.57), Body tattooing(AOR = 3.54, 95%CI: 1.65–7.58), Contact history with HBV patients(AOR = 8.38, 95%CI: 3.89,-18.06), HIV positivity(AOR = 10.04, 95%CI: 3.91–25.75), dental procedure(AOR = 3.64, 95%CI: 1.92, 6.92), Family history of HBV(AOR = 6.37, 95%CI: 3.83–10.6), History of blood transfusion(AOR = 6.32, 95%CI: 4.11–9.74), History of hospitalization(AOR = 3.99, 95%CI: 2.47–6.45), Multiple sexual partner(AOR = 7.91, 95%CI: 5.31–11.8) and Traditional tonsillectomy(AOR = 3.81, 95%CI: 1.92–7.57) were associated factors for hepatitis B infection among pregnant women. Abstract Background Hepatitis B virus infection among pregnant women is a significant public health problem in Ethiopia. Although primary studies on HBV infection among pregnant women have been conducted across different regions in Ethiopia, a synthesized pooled estimate and associated factors remain fragmented. Different databases (Google Scholar, PubMed, Embase, Web of Science, Scopus, Cochrane Library, and African Journal Online) were searched to identify relevant studies published between Jan...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review aimed to synthesise the available information and make an accurate estimate of hepatitis B virus infection among pregnant women in Ethiopia, with a focus on prevalence and associated factors.
Evidence base and method
A systematic review and meta-analysis were conducted according to the PRISMA 2020 guidelines. Studies were identified from multiple databases including Google Scholar, PubMed, Embase, Web of Science, Scopus, Cochrane Library, and African Journal Online, covering publications from January 1, 2021, to December 25, 2025. The Joanna Briggs Institute tool was used to assess methodological quality, and the random effect DerSimonian-Liard model was applied to estimate pooled prevalence. Heterogeneity was assessed using I2, and publication bias was evaluated using Egger’s test and a funnel plot.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled prevalence of hepatitis B infection among pregnant women in Ethiopia was estimated at 7.3% (95% CI: 6.3–8.4%), with significant heterogeneity between studies (I2 = 76.25%, p < 0.001). Several factors were identified as being associated with HBV infection, including history of abortion, body tattooing, contact history with HBV patients, HIV positivity, dental procedures, family history of HBV, history of blood transfusion, history of hospitalization, multiple sexual partners, and traditional tonsillectomy.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that the available studies on HBV infection among pregnant women in Ethiopia are fragmented, and there is no consensus on the pooled estimate of prevalence. The significant heterogeneity (I2 = 76.25%) suggests variability in study designs and populations, which may affect the reliability of the pooled estimate. Additionally, the review notes that the evidence is limited in certain areas, such as the geographic distribution of risk factors and the impact of specific interventions.
Research and practice implications
Early detection of HBV infection in pregnant women, awareness creation on the mode of transmission, and the implementation of evidence-based control measures are recommended. Further research is needed to address the evidence gaps, particularly in understanding the mechanisms of transmission and the effectiveness of interventions in reducing HBV prevalence among pregnant women.

中国在抗病毒药物治疗药物创新、个体化和翻译中的新兴作用

The Emerging Role of China in the Innovation, Individualization, and Translation of Antiviral Therapeutics for Chronic Hepatitis B
作者： Dan-Dan Weng, Zhu-Jun Cao, Qing Xie
摘要中文翻译摘要 慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)仍然是全球主要的健康负担,而功能性治愈——持续的乙肝病毒表面抗原(HBsAg)丢失,无论是否伴随HBsAg抗体血清转换,以及在停止治疗治疗后HBV DNA不可检测——仍较为罕见。中国正在从疾病控制向治疗药物创新和证据生成转变。Bepirovirsen为有限疗程治疗提供了首个III期试验基准,但大多数基于生物标志物选择的患者未能实现功能性治愈。中国起源和主导的项目正在扩展至抗原降低和免疫调节机制,结果既有积极的也有消极的。本文从视角探讨了中国在乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)药物开发、个体化治疗治疗、监管评估、真实世界转化以及终点优化方面的贡献。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于中国在抗病毒药物治疗药物创新、个体化和翻译中的作用。核心问题是:中国如何在慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)的HBV 治疗治疗药物开发和应用中发挥作用,特别是在涉及持续HBsAg丢失、血清转换以及停止治疗治疗后HBV DNA不可检测的功能性治愈背景下。
证据基础与方法
本综述是一篇观点文章,探讨了中国在HBV药物开发、个体化治疗治疗、监管评估、真实世界转化以及终点优化方面的贡献。它并未报告特定数据库、检索日期或研究纳入标准,也未包含对研究结果的荟萃分析或综合估计。
共识与结论
综述指出,bepirovirsen为有限疗程治疗提供了首个III期试验基准,但大多数基于生物标志物选择的患者未能实现功能性治愈。同时,综述还提到中国正在从疾病控制向治疗药物创新和证据生成转变,相关项目正在扩展至抗原降低和免疫调节机制,结果既有积极的也有消极的。
争议与缺口
综述指出,对于功能性治愈的定义和实现标准尚无共识,且关于HBV 治疗的长期疗效和安全性证据有限。此外,尽管中国主导的项目正在扩展,但这些项目的证据基础仍不完整,需要进一步研究。
科研与实践启示
综述建议未来研究应聚焦于优化治疗的终点指标、改进个体化治疗治疗策略,并加强HBV治疗药物在真实世界中的转化应用。同时,强调需要更坚实的证据生成和监管评估,以支持针对慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)的有效抗病毒药物治疗药物的开发。

The Emerging Role of China in the Innovation, Individualization, and Translation of Antiviral Therapeutics for Chronic Hepatitis B

Authors: Dan-Dan Weng, Zhu-Jun Cao, Qing Xie
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a major global health burden, and functional cure—sustained hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss, with or without antibody to HBsAg seroconversion, and undetectable hepatitis B virus (HBV) DNA after treatment cessation—remains uncommon. China is moving from disease control toward therapeutic innovation and evidence generation. Bepirovirsen provides the first Phase III benchmark for finite therapy, but most biomarker‐selected patients did not achieve functional cure. China‐origin and China‐led programs are expanding across antigen‐lowering and immune‐modulating mechanisms, with both promising and negative results. This Perspective examines China's contribution to HBV drug development, individualized treatment, regulatory evaluation, real‐world translation, and endpoint refinement.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the role of China in the innovation, individualization, and translation of antiviral therapeutics for chronic hepatitis B (CHB). The central question is how China contributes to the development and application of HBV treatments, particularly in the context of functional cure, which involves sustained HBsAg loss, seroconversion, and undetectable HBV DNA after treatment cessation.
Evidence base and method
The review is a Perspective article that examines China's contributions to HBV drug development, individualized treatment, regulatory evaluation, real-world translation, and endpoint refinement. It does not report on specific databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria for studies, and it does not include a meta-analysis or pooled estimate of study results.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that bepirovirsen provides the first Phase III benchmark for finite therapy, but most biomarker-selected patients did not achieve functional cure. It also notes that China is transitioning from disease control to therapeutic innovation and evidence generation, with programs expanding across antigen-lowering and immune-modulating mechanisms, yielding both promising and negative results.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a lack of consensus on the definition and achievement of functional cure, as well as limited evidence on the long-term efficacy and safety of HBV treatments. It also points out that while China-led programs are expanding, the evidence base for these initiatives remains incomplete and requires further investigation.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on refining treatment endpoints, improving individualized treatment strategies, and enhancing real-world translation of HBV therapeutics. It also emphasizes the need for more robust evidence generation and regulatory evaluation to support the development of effective antiviral therapies for chronic hepatitis B.
